main,mainBody,tabBar,bottomBar,fpsText = nil,nil,nil,nil,nil
panels,panelSetters,tabButtons={},{},{}
actionSettingsPanel,actionSettingsBody,tpSpeedSettingsPanel,tpSpeedSettingsBody = nil,nil,nil,nil
activeFeaturesPanel,activeFeaturesBody = nil,nil
BoundToggles={}

stealProgressBarGui = nil

-- Pre-declare helper functions made global to free local registers in outer scope

_G.ShowStealProgressBar = function(targetName, duration)
    local ExploitGui = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui")
    
    if stealProgressBarGui then
        pcall(function() stealProgressBarGui:Destroy() end)
    end
    for _, root in ipairs({ExploitGui, playerGui}) do
        pcall(function()
            local oldBar = root and root:FindFirstChild("SXE_StealProgressBar")
            if oldBar then oldBar:Destroy() end
        end)
    end
    
    local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    sg.Name = "SXE_StealProgressBar"
    sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
    sg.Parent = ExploitGui
    stealProgressBarGui = sg
    
    local barWidth = 338
    local barHeight = 82
    
    local container = Instance.new("Frame")
    container.Name = "StealProgressCard"
    container.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(barWidth, barHeight)
    container.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -barWidth/2, 1, -220)
    container.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.MainBackground
    container.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    container.BorderSizePixel = 0
    container.Parent = registerScreenGui(sg)
    corner(container, 18)
    if _G.__SXEStyleGlowBorder then _G.__SXEStyleGlowBorder(container) end
    local containerGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", container)
    containerGradient.Rotation = 15
    containerGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.MainBackground), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, Theme.Panel), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.MainBackground)})
    
    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    title.Size = UDim2.new(0.72, -14, 0, 20)
    title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 13)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Text = "STEAL TRANSFER  •  " .. (targetName or "Brainrot"):upper()
    title.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    title.TextSize = 11
    title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    title.Parent = container
    
    local pctLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    pctLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.28, -18, 0, 20)
    pctLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.72, 0, 0, 13)
    pctLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    pctLabel.Text = "0%"
    pctLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.AccentLight
    pctLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    pctLabel.TextSize = 13
    pctLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    pctLabel.Parent = container
    
    local track = Instance.new("Frame")
    track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 12)
    track.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 48)
    track.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SliderBg
    track.BorderSizePixel = 0
    track.Parent = container
    corner(track, 3)
    local progressStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", track)
    progressStroke.Color = Theme.Stroke
    progressStroke.Thickness = 1
    progressStroke.Transparency = 0.45
    progressStroke:SetAttribute("SXEPreserveStroke", true)
    
    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.Parent = track
    corner(fill, 3)
    
    local fillGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", fill)
    fillGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.AccentLight),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.Accent)
    })

    track.ClipsDescendants = true
    scanLine = Instance.new("Frame", track)
    scanLine.Name = "ComplexScanLine"
    scanLine.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    scanLine.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 1.9, 0)
    scanLine.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
    scanLine.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    scanLine.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
    scanLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
    scanLine.ZIndex = 20
    corner(scanLine, 4)
    scanLineGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", scanLine)
    scanLineGrad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
    })
    
    local safeDuration = tonumber(duration) or 5
    if safeDuration <= 0.01 then safeDuration = 5 end
    local startTick = tick()
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while sg and sg.Parent do
            local elapsed = tick() - startTick
            local t = math.clamp(elapsed / safeDuration, 0, 1)
            local ratio = 1 - (1 - t)^3
            
            pcall(function()
                pctLabel.Text = math.floor(ratio * 100) .. "%"
                title.Text = "STEAL TRANSFER" .. string.rep(".", (math.floor(tick() * 4) % 3) + 1) .. "  •  " .. (targetName or "Brainrot"):upper()
                fill.Size = UDim2.new(ratio, 0, 1, 0)
                fillGrad.Rotation = 20 * math.sin(tick() * 2.2)
                if scanLine then
                    scanLine.Position = UDim2.new(0.5 + 0.48 * math.sin(tick() * 5.5), 0, 0.5, 0)
                    scanLine.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08 + 0.2 * math.abs(math.sin(tick() * 8))
                    scanLineGrad.Rotation = 25 * math.sin(tick() * 2)
                end
            end)
            
            if t >= 1 then break end
            task.wait()
        end
    end)
end

_G.HideStealProgressBar = function()
    local targetGui = stealProgressBarGui
    if targetGui then
        stealProgressBarGui = nil
        pcall(function()
            local container = targetGui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Frame")
            if container then
                tw(container, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, 0.15)
                for _, child in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
                    if child:IsA("TextLabel") then
                        tw(child, {TextTransparency = 1}, 0.15)
                    elseif child:IsA("Frame") then
                        tw(child, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, 0.15)
                    elseif child:IsA("UIStroke") then
                        tw(child, {Transparency = 1}, 0.15)
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.16)
            end
            targetGui:Destroy()
        end)
    end
end

-- Assign helper implementations to pre-declared outer local variables
corner = function(o,r) local c=Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,r); c.Parent=o; return c end
stroke = function(o,col,th,tr) return nil end
tw = function(o,p,t) TweenService:Create(o,TweenInfo.new(t or 0.14,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),p):Play() end
-- Faint hairline edge only (NOT a neon glow).
addOutline = function(f) return nil end
-- PORK HUB 2.0 shared widget bits (GLOBAL -> no local-cap cost).
-- accentBar: the live left-edge indicator every card row carries; bright accent
-- when active, dim when idle. Returned so builders can light it on state change.
accentBar = function(parent, active) local b=Instance.new("Frame"); b.Name="AccentBar"; b.Size=UDim2.new(0,3,0,18); b.Position=UDim2.new(0,5,0.5,-9); b.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0,0); b.BackgroundColor3=active and Theme.Accent or Theme.RowHover; b.BackgroundTransparency=active and 0 or 0.68; b.BorderSizePixel=0; b.Visible=true; b.Parent=parent; corner(b,3); return b end
setAccentBar = function(bar, active) if bar then bar.Visible=true; tw(bar,{BackgroundColor3=active and Theme.Accent or Theme.RowHover,BackgroundTransparency=active and 0 or 0.68,Size=UDim2.new(0,3,0,active and 22 or 14),Position=UDim2.new(0,5,0.5,active and -11 or -7)},0.16) end end
-- card: the new base row -- soft rounded panel + faint accent hairline, replaces
-- the old flat SXE rows. Returns the frame; callers add the accent bar + content.
card = function(parent, h) local f=Instance.new("Frame"); f.Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,h or 42); f.BackgroundColor3=Theme.Row; f.BackgroundTransparency=0.04; f.BorderSizePixel=0; f.Parent=parent; corner(f,11); local g=Instance.new("UIGradient"); g.Color=ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,Theme.RowHover),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,Theme.Row)}); g.Rotation=0; g.Parent=f; return f end


-- Smooth hover/press motion shared by buttons, dropdown choices and multi-option rows.
function addPorkHover(button, baseColor, hoverColor, scaleAmount)
    if not button or not button:IsA("GuiButton") or button:GetAttribute("PorkHoverAttached") then return button end
    button:SetAttribute("PorkHoverAttached", true)
    local scale = button:FindFirstChild("PorkHoverScale") or Instance.new("UIScale")
    scale.Name = "PorkHoverScale"
    scale.Scale = 1
    scale.Parent = button
    local hoverScale = scaleAmount or 1.025

    button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if hoverColor then tw(button, {BackgroundColor3 = hoverColor}, 0.14) end
        tw(scale, {Scale = hoverScale}, 0.14)
    end)
    button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if baseColor then tw(button, {BackgroundColor3 = baseColor}, 0.14) end
        tw(scale, {Scale = 1}, 0.14)
    end)
    button.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
        tw(scale, {Scale = 0.975}, 0.07)
    end)
    button.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
        tw(scale, {Scale = hoverScale}, 0.09)
    end)
    return button
end


-- Optional theme-colored border lines. Two preserved strokes create a crisp edge
-- plus a soft outer halo without restoring the old outlines on every small widget.
function _G.__SXEStyleGlowBorder(frame)
    if not frame or not frame:IsA("GuiObject") then return end

    local edge = frame:FindFirstChild("SXEGlowBorder")
    if not edge then
        edge = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        edge.Name = "SXEGlowBorder"
        edge:SetAttribute("SXEPreserveStroke", true)
        edge:SetAttribute("SXEGlowBorder", true)
        edge.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        edge.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Round
        edge.Parent = frame
        local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        grad.Name = "GlowGradient"
        grad.Rotation = 18
        grad.Parent = edge
    end

    local halo = frame:FindFirstChild("SXEGlowHalo")
    if not halo then
        halo = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        halo.Name = "SXEGlowHalo"
        halo:SetAttribute("SXEPreserveStroke", true)
        halo:SetAttribute("SXEGlowBorder", true)
        halo.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        halo.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Round
        halo.Parent = frame
        local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        grad.Name = "GlowGradient"
        grad.Rotation = -18
        grad.Parent = halo
    end

    local enabled = Config and Config.GlowBorders == true
    local sequence = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.Accent),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Theme.AccentLight),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.Accent),
    })
    edge.Color = Theme.AccentLight
    edge.Thickness = enabled and 1.8 or 1
    edge.Transparency = enabled and 0.08 or 1
    halo.Color = Theme.Accent
    halo.Thickness = enabled and 4.5 or 1
    halo.Transparency = enabled and 0.72 or 1
    local edgeGradient = edge:FindFirstChild("GlowGradient")
    local haloGradient = halo:FindFirstChild("GlowGradient")
    if edgeGradient then edgeGradient.Color = sequence end
    if haloGradient then haloGradient.Color = sequence end
end

function _G.__SXEApplyGlowBorders()
    local seen = {}
    local function style(frame)
        if frame and not seen[frame] then
            seen[frame] = true
            pcall(_G.__SXEStyleGlowBorder, frame)
        end
    end
    style(main)
    style(actionSettingsPanel)
    style(tpSpeedSettingsPanel)
    style(activeFeaturesPanel)
    style(targetHud)
    for _, frame in pairs(panels or {}) do style(frame) end

    for _, root in ipairs({
        playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("SXE_StealProgressBar"),
        playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("AutoStealCurrentTargetHUD")
    }) do
        if root then
            for _, inst in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                if inst:IsA("Frame") and (inst.Name == "StealProgressCard" or inst.Name == "CurrentTargetHUD") then
                    style(inst)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function fitPorkPanel(frame)
    return withUICapability(function()
        if not frame or not frame.Parent then return end
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end

        local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
        local globalScale = math.max(getGlobalScale(), 0.01)
        local panelScaleObject = frame:FindFirstChild("PorkPanel_SizeScale")
        local panelScale = panelScaleObject and panelScaleObject.Scale or 1
        local combinedScale = math.max(globalScale * panelScale, 0.01)

        local maxWidth = math.max(220, (viewport.X - 24) / combinedScale)
        local maxHeight = math.max(180, (viewport.Y - 24) / combinedScale)
        local currentWidth = frame.AbsoluteSize.X / combinedScale
        local currentHeight = frame.AbsoluteSize.Y / combinedScale
        if currentWidth > maxWidth or currentHeight > maxHeight then
            frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
                math.min(currentWidth, maxWidth),
                math.min(currentHeight, maxHeight)
            )
        end

        local absPos = frame.AbsolutePosition
        local absSize = frame.AbsoluteSize
        local deltaX, deltaY = 0, 0
        if absPos.X < 8 then deltaX = 8 - absPos.X end
        if absPos.Y < 8 then deltaY = 8 - absPos.Y end
        if absPos.X + absSize.X > viewport.X - 8 then
            deltaX = (viewport.X - 8) - (absPos.X + absSize.X)
        end
        if absPos.Y + absSize.Y > viewport.Y - 8 then
            deltaY = (viewport.Y - 8) - (absPos.Y + absSize.Y)
        end

        if deltaX ~= 0 or deltaY ~= 0 then
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(
                frame.Position.X.Scale,
                frame.Position.X.Offset + (deltaX / globalScale),
                frame.Position.Y.Scale,
                frame.Position.Y.Offset + (deltaY / globalScale)
            )
        end
    end)
end

_G.PorkHubFitAllPanels = function()
    local roots = {main, actionSettingsPanel, tpSpeedSettingsPanel, apOuter}
    if panels then
        for _, panel in pairs(panels) do
            if typeof(panel) == "Instance" and panel:IsA("GuiObject") then
                table.insert(roots, panel)
            end
        end
    end
    local seen = {}
    for _, panel in ipairs(roots) do
        if panel and not seen[panel] then
            seen[panel] = true
            pcall(fitPorkPanel, panel)
        end
    end
end
function clearBody(body) for _,c in ipairs(body:GetChildren()) do if not c:IsA("UIListLayout") and not c:IsA("UIPadding") then c:Destroy() end end end

function openAnim(f) if not f then return end; local us=f:FindFirstChild("SXEScale") or Instance.new("UIScale"); us.Name="SXEScale"; us.Parent=f
    local tgt=f.Position; f.Visible=true; us.Scale=0.92; f.Position=UDim2.new(tgt.X.Scale,tgt.X.Offset,tgt.Y.Scale,tgt.Y.Offset+18); tw(us,{Scale=1},0.20); tw(f,{Position=tgt},0.20) end
function closeAnim(f) if not f then return end; f.Visible = false end

makeDraggable = function(frame, handle, saveName)
    local dragging = false
    local dragStart
    local startPosition

    handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        withUICapability(function()
            if UI.Locked then return end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = input.Position
                startPosition = frame.Position
            end
        end)
    end)

    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging or UI.Locked then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end

        withUICapability(function()
            if not frame or not frame.Parent or not startPosition or not dragStart then return end
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            local scale = math.max(getGlobalScale(), 0.01)
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(
                startPosition.X.Scale,
                startPosition.X.Offset + (delta.X / scale),
                startPosition.Y.Scale,
                startPosition.Y.Offset + (delta.Y / scale)
            )
        end)
    end)

    UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        if not dragging then return end

        dragging = false
        withUICapability(function()
            fitPorkPanel(frame)
            if saveName and frame and frame.Parent then
                rememberPosition(saveName, frame)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

makeResizable = function(frame, minSize, panelName)
    if not frame then return nil end
    Config.sizes = Config.sizes or {}
    Config.positions = Config.positions or {}
    minSize = minSize or UDim2.fromOffset(220, 180)

    local handle = Instance.new("TextButton")
    handle.Name = "ResizeHandle"
    handle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
    handle.Position = UDim2.new(1, -7, 1, -7)
    handle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22)
    handle.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftButton
    handle.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    handle.Text = "↘"
    handle.TextColor3 = Theme.Dim
    handle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    handle.TextSize = 12
    handle.AutoButtonColor = false
    handle.ZIndex = 150
    handle.Parent = frame
    corner(handle, 7)

    local dragging = false
    local startMouse
    local startSize
    local moveConn
    local endConn

    local function combinedScale()
        local panelScaleObject = frame:FindFirstChild("PorkPanel_SizeScale")
        local panelScale = panelScaleObject and panelScaleObject.Scale or 1
        return math.max(getGlobalScale() * panelScale, 0.01)
    end

    local function logicalSize()
        if frame.Size.X.Scale == 0 and frame.Size.Y.Scale == 0 then
            return Vector2.new(frame.Size.X.Offset, frame.Size.Y.Offset)
        end
        return frame.AbsoluteSize / combinedScale()
    end

    local function finish()
        if not dragging then return end
        dragging = false
        if moveConn then moveConn:Disconnect(); moveConn = nil end
        if endConn then endConn:Disconnect(); endConn = nil end

        withUICapability(function()
            fitPorkPanel(frame)
            if panelName and frame and frame.Parent then
                local size = logicalSize()
                Config.sizes[panelName] = {
                    x = math.floor(size.X + 0.5),
                    y = math.floor(size.Y + 0.5),
                }
                Config.positions[panelName] = serializePos(frame.Position)
                saveConfig()
            end
        end)
    end

    handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if UI.Locked then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        local beganOkay = withUICapability(function()
            dragging = true
            startMouse = input.Position
            startSize = logicalSize()
        end)
        if not beganOkay then
            dragging = false
            return
        end

        moveConn = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(changed)
            if not dragging then return end
            if changed.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and changed.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            withUICapability(function()
                if not frame or not frame.Parent then return end
                local scaleValue = combinedScale()
                local delta = (changed.Position - startMouse) / scaleValue
                local minX = math.max(minSize.X.Offset, 180)
                local minY = math.max(minSize.Y.Offset, 140)
                local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1366, 768)
                local maxX = math.max(minX, (viewport.X - 24) / scaleValue)
                local maxY = math.max(minY, (viewport.Y - 24) / scaleValue)
                frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
                    math.clamp(startSize.X + delta.X, minX, maxX),
                    math.clamp(startSize.Y + delta.Y, minY, maxY)
                )
            end)
        end)

        endConn = UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(ended)
            if ended.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or ended.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then finish() end
        end)
    end)

    handle.MouseEnter:Connect(function() handle.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftButtonHover; handle.TextColor3 = Theme.Text end)
    handle.MouseLeave:Connect(function() handle.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftButton; handle.TextColor3 = Theme.Dim end)
    return handle
end

function addPanelScaleControls(frame, panelName)
    if not frame or not panelName then return end
    Config.PanelScales = Config.PanelScales or {}
    local saved = tonumber(Config.PanelScales[panelName]) or 1.00
    saved = math.clamp(saved, 0.70, 1.50)
    Config.PanelScales[panelName] = saved

    local scaleObj = frame:FindFirstChild("PorkPanel_SizeScale")
    if not scaleObj then
        scaleObj = Instance.new("UIScale")
        scaleObj.Name = "PorkPanel_SizeScale"
        scaleObj.Parent = frame
    end
    scaleObj.Scale = saved

    local minus = Instance.new("TextButton")
    minus.Name = "PanelScaleMinus"
    if minus then minus.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0) end
    minus.Position = UDim2.new(1, -86, 0, 8)
    minus.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 22)
    minus.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftButton
    minus.BackgroundTransparency = 0.02
    minus.Text = "-"
    minus.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    minus.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    minus.TextSize = 14
    minus.AutoButtonColor = false
    minus.ZIndex = 120
    minus.Parent = frame
    corner(minus, 7)
    stroke(minus, Theme.AccentLight, 1, 0.55)

    local pct = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    pct.Name = "PanelScalePercent"
    if pct then pct.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0) end
    pct.Position = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 8)
    pct.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 22)
    pct.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    pct.Text = tostring(math.floor((saved * 100) + 0.5)) .. "%"
    pct.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    pct.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    pct.TextSize = 11
    pct.ZIndex = 120
    pct.Parent = frame

    local plus = Instance.new("TextButton")
    plus.Name = "PanelScalePlus"
    if plus then plus.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0) end
    plus.Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 8)
    plus.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 22)
    plus.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftButton
    plus.BackgroundTransparency = 0.02
    plus.Text = "+"
    plus.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    plus.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    plus.TextSize = 14
    plus.AutoButtonColor = false
    plus.ZIndex = 120
    plus.Parent = frame
    corner(plus, 7)
    stroke(plus, Theme.AccentLight, 1, 0.55)

    local function setScale(v)
        v = math.clamp(v, 0.70, 1.50)
        Config.PanelScales[panelName] = v
        scaleObj.Scale = v
        pct.Text = tostring(math.floor((v * 100) + 0.5)) .. "%"
        task.defer(function()
            fitPorkPanel(frame)
            if frame and frame.Parent then
                Config.positions = Config.positions or {}
                Config.positions[panelName] = serializePos(frame.Position)
            end
            if saveConfig then saveConfig() end
        end)
    end

    minus.MouseEnter:Connect(function() minus.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftButtonHover end)
    minus.MouseLeave:Connect(function() minus.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftButton end)
    plus.MouseEnter:Connect(function() plus.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftButtonHover end)
    plus.MouseLeave:Connect(function() plus.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftButton end)
    minus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setScale((Config.PanelScales[panelName] or 1) - 0.05) end)
    plus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setScale((Config.PanelScales[panelName] or 1) + 0.05) end)
end

function makeHeader(f, t, isMain)
    local h = Instance.new("Frame")
    h.Name = "Header"
    h.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, isMain and 62 or 54)
    h.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel
    h.BackgroundTransparency = isMain and 0.08 or 0.12
    h.BorderSizePixel = 0
    h.Parent = f

    local parts = {}
    for part in string.gmatch(t or "PORK HUB", "([^\n]+)") do table.insert(parts, part) end

    makePorkBadge(
        h,
        UDim2.fromOffset(isMain and 36 or 30, isMain and 36 or 30),
        UDim2.fromOffset(12, isMain and 13 or 12),
        isMain and 11 or 9,
        "PorkLogoBadge"
    )

    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -160, 0, 22)
    title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(isMain and 58 or 52, isMain and 11 or 8)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Text = parts[1] or "PORK HUB"
    title.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    title.TextSize = isMain and 16 or 13
    title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    title.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    title.Parent = h

    local subtitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    subtitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -160, 0, 16)
    subtitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(isMain and 58 or 52, isMain and 33 or 29)
    subtitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    subtitle.Text = parts[2] or (isMain and "CONTROL CENTER" or "UTILITY PANEL")
    subtitle.TextColor3 = Theme.Dim
    subtitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    subtitle.TextSize = isMain and 10 or 9
    subtitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    subtitle.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    subtitle.Parent = h

    local divider = Instance.new("Frame")
    divider.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 1)
    divider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -1)
    divider.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.RowHover
    divider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
    divider.BorderSizePixel = 0
    divider.Parent = h

    makeDraggable(f, h, t)
    return h
end

function makeMainPanel(t, size, pos)
    local f = Instance.new("Frame")
    f.Name = "Main"
    f.Size = size
    f.Position = pos
    f.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.MainBackground
    f.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    f.BorderSizePixel = 0
    f.ClipsDescendants = false
    f.Parent = gui
    corner(f, 20)
    if _G.__SXEStyleGlowBorder then _G.__SXEStyleGlowBorder(f) end
    makeHeader(f, t, true)

    local side = Instance.new("Frame")
    side.Name = "SidebarSurface"
    side.Size = UDim2.new(0, 154, 1, -118)
    side.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 68)
    side.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel
    side.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
    side.BorderSizePixel = 0
    side.Parent = f
    corner(side, 14)

    local body = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    body.Name = "Content"
    body.Size = UDim2.new(1, -188, 1, -124)
    body.Position = UDim2.new(0, 176, 0, 68)
    body.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Background
    body.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
    body.BorderSizePixel = 0
    body.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    body.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Accent
    body.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    body.Active = true
    body.Parent = f
    corner(body, 14)

    local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 10)
    padding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 10)
    padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
    padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)
    padding.Parent = body

    local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
    lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    lay.Parent = body
    lay:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
        body.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, lay.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 16)
    end)

    if Config.sizes and Config.sizes[t] then
        f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Config.sizes[t].x, Config.sizes[t].y)
    end
    addPanelScaleControls(f, t)
    makeResizable(f, UDim2.fromOffset(560, 440), t)
    task.defer(function() fitPorkPanel(f) end)
    return f, body
end

function makeQuickPanel(t, size, pos)
    local f = Instance.new("Frame")
    f.Name = (t or "Panel"):gsub("[^%w]+", "_")
    f.Size = size
    f.Position = pos
    f.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.MainBackground
    f.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    f.BorderSizePixel = 0
    f.ClipsDescendants = false
    f.Parent = gui
    corner(f, 18)
    if _G.__SXEStyleGlowBorder then _G.__SXEStyleGlowBorder(f) end
    makeHeader(f, t, false)

    local body = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    body.Name = "PanelContent"
    body.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -66)
    body.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 60)
    body.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Background
    body.BackgroundTransparency = 0.14
    body.BorderSizePixel = 0
    body.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    body.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Accent
    body.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    body.Active = true
    body.Parent = f
    corner(body, 13)

    local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 7)
    padding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 7)
    padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 7)
    padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 7)
    padding.Parent = body

    local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 7)
    lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    lay.Parent = body
    lay:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
        body.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, lay.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 14)
    end)

    if Config.sizes and Config.sizes[t] then
        f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Config.sizes[t].x, Config.sizes[t].y)
    end
    addPanelScaleControls(f, t)
    makeResizable(f, UDim2.fromOffset(250, 210), t)
    task.defer(function() fitPorkPanel(f) end)
    return f, body
end

-- Global UI transparency slider support. Higher value = more see-through.
-- FIXED: every object stores its ORIGINAL transparency once, then every slider
-- update recalculates from that original value. This stops transparency stacking,
-- so dragging to 58 and back to 0 restores the real original UI transparency.
SXEUIOriginalTransparencies = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})

function _sxeBlendTransparency(original, alpha)
    original = tonumber(original) or 0
    alpha = math.clamp(tonumber(alpha) or 0, 0, 0.85)
    return math.clamp(original + ((1 - original) * alpha), 0, 1)
end

function _sxeRememberTransparency(inst)
    if SXEUIOriginalTransparencies[inst] then return SXEUIOriginalTransparencies[inst] end

    local data = {}
    if inst:IsA("GuiObject") then
        data.BackgroundTransparency = inst.BackgroundTransparency
    end
    if inst:IsA("ImageLabel") or inst:IsA("ImageButton") then
        data.ImageTransparency = inst.ImageTransparency
    end
    if inst:IsA("UIStroke") then
        data.Transparency = inst.Transparency
    end

    SXEUIOriginalTransparencies[inst] = data
    return data
end

function applySXEUITransparency(value)
    local alpha = math.clamp(tonumber(value or (Config and Config.UITransparency)) or 0, 0, 0.85)
    if Config then Config.UITransparency = alpha end

    local function applyObj(inst)
        if inst:IsA("Frame") or inst:IsA("ScrollingFrame") or inst:IsA("TextButton") or inst:IsA("TextBox") or inst:IsA("ImageButton") or inst:IsA("ImageLabel") then
            local data = _sxeRememberTransparency(inst)
            local isToggleShell =
                inst:IsA("TextButton")
                and inst.Text == ""
                and inst.AbsoluteSize.X >= 35 and inst.AbsoluteSize.X <= 70
                and inst.AbsoluteSize.Y >= 18 and inst.AbsoluteSize.Y <= 34
                and inst:FindFirstChildOfClass("Frame") ~= nil

            local isToggleKnob =
                inst:IsA("Frame")
                and inst.Parent
                and inst.Parent:IsA("TextButton")
                and inst.Parent.Text == ""
                and inst.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Frame") == inst

            local keepOpaque = inst.Name == "WhiteSliderKnob" or isToggleShell or isToggleKnob
            if data.BackgroundTransparency ~= nil then
                if keepOpaque then
                    -- Toggle tracks/knobs should stay fully solid even when global UI transparency is high.
                    inst.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                else
                    inst.BackgroundTransparency = _sxeBlendTransparency(data.BackgroundTransparency, alpha)
                end
            end
            if (inst:IsA("ImageLabel") or inst:IsA("ImageButton")) and data.ImageTransparency ~= nil then
                inst.ImageTransparency = _sxeBlendTransparency(data.ImageTransparency, alpha)
            end
        elseif inst:IsA("UIStroke") then
            local data = _sxeRememberTransparency(inst)
            local p = inst.Parent
            local isToggleStroke =
                p and p:IsA("TextButton")
                and p.Text == ""
                and p.AbsoluteSize.X >= 35 and p.AbsoluteSize.X <= 70
                and p.AbsoluteSize.Y >= 18 and p.AbsoluteSize.Y <= 34
            -- Keep outlines visible. Toggle outlines are forced fully solid.
            if isToggleStroke then
                inst.Transparency = 0
            elseif data.Transparency ~= nil then
                inst.Transparency = data.Transparency
            else
                inst.Transparency = 0
            end
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(inst:GetChildren()) do
            applyObj(child)
        end
    end

    pcall(function() if gui_sg then applyObj(gui_sg) end end)
    pcall(function()
        local otherSg = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("XiAdminPanel")
        if otherSg then applyObj(otherSg) end
    end)
end
_G.applySXEUITransparency = applySXEUITransparency

function makeSyncStateRow(parent,text,toggleName,callback)
    regToggle(toggleName,getToggle(toggleName))
    local iv=getToggle(toggleName)
    local row=card(parent,38)
    local bar=accentBar(row,iv)
    local label=Instance.new("TextLabel"); label.Size=UDim2.new(1,-96,1,0); label.Position=UDim2.new(0,14,0,0); label.BackgroundTransparency=1; label.Text=text; label.TextColor3=Theme.Text; label.Font=Enum.Font.GothamSemibold; label.TextSize=12; label.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; label.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd; label.Parent=row
    local btn=Instance.new("TextButton"); btn.Name="WhiteTextBtn"; btn.Size=UDim2.new(0,74,0,28); btn.Position=UDim2.new(1,-80,0.5,-14); btn.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1); btn.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; btn.TextSize=12; btn.AutoButtonColor=false; btn.Parent=row; corner(btn,8); stroke(btn,Theme.AccentLight,1,0.4)
    local function refresh(val) btn.BackgroundColor3=val and Theme.Green or Theme.ToggleOff2; btn.Text=val and "ON" or "OFF"; setAccentBar(bar,val) end
    refresh(iv); onToggleChanged(toggleName,function(val) refresh(val) end)
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() local nv=not getToggle(toggleName); setToggle(toggleName,nv); if callback then callback(nv) end end)
    return function(ns,fire) if typeof(ns)=="boolean" then setToggle(toggleName,ns); if fire~=false and callback then callback(ns) end end end, label
end

function makeSyncMainToggle(parent,text,toggleName,callback)
    regToggle(toggleName,getToggle(toggleName))
    local iv=getToggle(toggleName)
    local row=card(parent,38)
    local bar=accentBar(row,iv)
    local l=Instance.new("TextLabel"); l.Size=UDim2.new(1,-70,1,0); l.Position=UDim2.new(0,14,0,0); l.BackgroundTransparency=1; l.Text=text; l.TextColor3=Theme.Text; l.Font=Enum.Font.GothamSemibold; l.TextSize=12; l.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; l.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd; l.Parent=row
    local toggle=Instance.new("TextButton"); toggle.Size=UDim2.new(0,46,0,24); toggle.Position=UDim2.new(1,-54,0.5,-12); toggle.Text=""; toggle.AutoButtonColor=false; toggle.Parent=row; corner(toggle,12); stroke(toggle,Theme.AccentLight,1,0)
    local dot=Instance.new("Frame"); dot.Size=UDim2.new(0,18,0,18); dot.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255); dot.Parent=toggle; corner(dot,20)
    local function refresh(val) tw(toggle,{BackgroundColor3=val and Theme.Green or Theme.ToggleOff},0.14); tw(dot,{Position=val and UDim2.new(1,-21,0.5,-9) or UDim2.new(0,3,0.5,-9)},0.14); setAccentBar(bar,val) end
    refresh(iv); dot.Position=iv and UDim2.new(1,-21,0.5,-9) or UDim2.new(0,3,0.5,-9)
    toggle.BackgroundColor3=iv and Theme.Green or Theme.ToggleOff
    onToggleChanged(toggleName,function(val) refresh(val) end)
    toggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() local nv=not getToggle(toggleName); setToggle(toggleName,nv); if callback then callback(nv) end end)
    BoundToggles[text]=function(ns,fire) if typeof(ns)=="boolean" then setToggle(toggleName,ns); if fire~=false and callback then callback(ns) end end end
    return BoundToggles[text]
end

function makeQuickButton(parent,text,callback,bg) local b=Instance.new("TextButton"); b.Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,38); b.BackgroundColor3=bg or Theme.SoftButton; b.BackgroundTransparency=0.02; b.Text=text; b.TextColor3=Theme.Text; b.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; b.TextSize=13; b.AutoButtonColor=false; b.Parent=parent; corner(b,9); stroke(b,Theme.AccentLight,1.25,0.4)
    local bar=accentBar(b,false)
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tw(b,{BackgroundColor3=bg or Theme.SoftButtonHover},0.12); setAccentBar(bar,true) end); b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tw(b,{BackgroundColor3=bg or Theme.SoftButton},0.12); setAccentBar(bar,false) end)
    addPorkHover(b, bg or Theme.SoftButton, bg or Theme.SoftButtonHover, 1.018)
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if callback then callback() end end); return b end

function makeQuickSlider(parent,text,min,max,default,callback,suffix) local holder=Instance.new("Frame"); holder.Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,52); holder.BackgroundColor3=Theme.Panel; holder.BackgroundTransparency=0.28; holder.BorderSizePixel=0; holder.Parent=parent; corner(holder,9); stroke(holder,Theme.AccentLight,1,0.72)
    accentBar(holder,true)
    local label=Instance.new("TextLabel"); label.Size=UDim2.new(1,-16,0,16); label.Position=UDim2.new(0,12,0,6); label.BackgroundTransparency=1; label.Text=text..": "..tostring(default)..(suffix or ""); label.TextColor3=Theme.Text; label.Font=Enum.Font.GothamSemibold; label.TextSize=11; label.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; label.Parent=holder
    local bar=Instance.new("Frame"); bar.Size=UDim2.new(1,-24,0,8); bar.Position=UDim2.new(0,12,0,30); bar.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SliderBg; bar.BorderSizePixel=0; bar.Parent=holder; corner(bar,10)
    local fill=Instance.new("Frame"); fill.Size=UDim2.new(math.clamp((default-min)/(max-min),0,1),0,1,0); fill.BackgroundColor3=Theme.Accent; fill.BorderSizePixel=0; fill.Parent=bar; corner(fill,10)
    local knob=Instance.new("Frame"); knob.Size=UDim2.new(0,16,0,16); knob.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5); knob.Position=UDim2.new(math.clamp((default-min)/(max-min),0,1),0,0.5,0); knob.Name = "WhiteSliderKnob"; knob.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255); knob.BorderSizePixel=0; knob.Parent=bar; corner(knob,20); stroke(knob,Theme.Accent,1.5,0)
    local dragging=false
    local function update(x) local rel=math.clamp((x-bar.AbsolutePosition.X)/bar.AbsoluteSize.X,0,1); local v=math.floor((min+(max-min)*rel)*10+0.5)/10; fill.Size=UDim2.new(rel,0,1,0); knob.Position=UDim2.new(rel,0,0.5,0); label.Text=text..": "..tostring(v)..(suffix or ""); if callback then callback(v) end end
    bar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging=true; update(i.Position.X) end end)
    UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i) if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging=false end end)
    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i) if dragging and (i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then update(i.Position.X) end end)
    local function setVal(v, silent)
        v = math.clamp(v, min, max)
        local rel = (v - min) / (max - min)
        local displayVal = math.floor(v * 10 + 0.5) / 10
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(rel, 0, 1, 0)
        knob.Position = UDim2.new(rel, 0, 0.5, 0)
        label.Text = text..": "..tostring(displayVal)..(suffix or "")
        if callback and not silent then callback(displayVal) end
    end
    return {Set = setVal}
end

function makeMainSliderWithInput(parent,text,min,max,default,callback,suffix)
    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1,-4,0,48)
    row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
    row.Parent = parent
    corner(row, 9); stroke(row,Theme.AccentLight,1,0.72)
    accentBar(row,true)

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size = UDim2.new(0.65,0,0,16)
    label.Position = UDim2.new(0,8,0,4)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = text
    label.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    label.TextSize = 10
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.Parent = row

    local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
    box.Size = UDim2.new(0.26,0,0,16)
    box.Position = UDim2.new(0.74,-8,0,4)
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.InputBg
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.Text = tostring(default)..(suffix or "")
    box.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    box.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    box.TextSize = 9
    box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    box.Parent = row
    corner(box,4)
    stroke(box, Theme.Stroke, 1, 0.4)

    local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
    if bar then bar.Size = UDim2.new(1,-16,0,8) end
    if bar then bar.Position = UDim2.new(0,8,0,28) end
    if bar then bar.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SliderBg end
    if bar then bar.BorderSizePixel = 0 end
    if bar then bar.Parent = row end
    corner(bar,10)

    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp((default-min)/(max-min),0,1),0,1,0)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.Parent = bar
    corner(fill,10)

    local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
    knob.Name = "WhiteSliderKnob"
    knob.Size = UDim2.new(0,16,0,16)
    knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5)
    knob.Position = UDim2.new(math.clamp((default-min)/(max-min),0,1),0,0.5,0)
    knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    knob.Parent = bar
    corner(knob,20)
    stroke(knob,Theme.Accent,1.5,0)

    local dragging = false
    local function updateValue(val, skipBox)
        val = math.clamp(val, min, max)
        val = math.floor(val * 100 + 0.5) / 100
        local rel = (val - min) / (max - min)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(rel,0,1,0)
        knob.Position = UDim2.new(rel,0,0.5,0)
        if not skipBox then
            box.Text = tostring(val)..(suffix or "")
        end
        if callback then callback(val) end
    end

    bar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            local rel = math.clamp((i.Position.X - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / bar.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
            updateValue(min + (max - min) * rel)
        end
    end)
    UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
        end
    end)
    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local rel = math.clamp((i.Position.X - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / bar.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
            updateValue(min + (max - min) * rel)
        end
    end)

    box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local raw = box.Text:gsub("[^%d%.]", "")
        local num = tonumber(raw)
        if num then
            updateValue(num)
        else
            box.Text = tostring(default)..(suffix or "")
        end
    end)
    return row
end

function makeMainButton(parent,text,callback,color) local b=Instance.new("TextButton"); b.Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,34); b.BackgroundColor3=color or Theme.Row; b.BackgroundTransparency=0.12; b.Text=text; b.TextColor3=Theme.Text; b.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; b.TextSize=12; b.AutoButtonColor=false; b.Parent=parent; corner(b,9); stroke(b,Theme.AccentLight,1.25,0.32)
    local bar=accentBar(b,false)
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tw(b,{BackgroundColor3=color or Theme.RowHover},0.12); setAccentBar(bar,true) end); b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tw(b,{BackgroundColor3=color or Theme.Row},0.12); setAccentBar(bar,false) end)
    addPorkHover(b, color or Theme.Row, color or Theme.RowHover, 1.018)
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if callback then callback() end end); return b end

function makeMainToggle(parent,text,enabled,callback)
    regToggle(text, enabled == true)
    setToggle(text, enabled == true, true)

    local row=card(parent,38)
    local state=getToggle(text)
    local bar=accentBar(row,state)
    local l=Instance.new("TextLabel"); l.Size=UDim2.new(1,-70,1,0); l.Position=UDim2.new(0,14,0,0); l.BackgroundTransparency=1; l.Text=text; l.TextColor3=Theme.Text; l.Font=Enum.Font.GothamSemibold; l.TextSize=12; l.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; l.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd; l.Parent=row
    local toggle=Instance.new("TextButton"); toggle.Size=UDim2.new(0,46,0,24); toggle.Position=UDim2.new(1,-54,0.5,-12); toggle.BackgroundColor3=state and Theme.Green or Theme.ToggleOff; toggle.BackgroundTransparency=0; toggle.Text=""; toggle.AutoButtonColor=false; toggle.Parent=row; corner(toggle,12); stroke(toggle,Theme.AccentLight,1,0)
    local dot=Instance.new("Frame"); dot.Size=UDim2.new(0,18,0,18); dot.Position=state and UDim2.new(1,-21,0.5,-9) or UDim2.new(0,3,0.5,-9); dot.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255); dot.BackgroundTransparency=0; dot.Parent=toggle; corner(dot,20)
    local function setState(ns,fire)
        state=ns and true or false
        toggle.BackgroundTransparency=0
        dot.BackgroundTransparency=0
        tw(toggle,{BackgroundColor3=state and Theme.Green or Theme.ToggleOff, BackgroundTransparency=0},0.14)
        tw(dot,{Position=state and UDim2.new(1,-21,0.5,-9) or UDim2.new(0,3,0.5,-9), BackgroundTransparency=0},0.14)
        setAccentBar(bar,state)
        if getToggle(text) ~= state then setToggle(text,state) end
        if fire~=false and callback then callback(state) end
    end
    onToggleChanged(text,function(val)
        if val ~= state then setState(val,false) end
    end)
    toggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setState(not state,true) end)
    BoundToggles[text]=function(ns,fire) if typeof(ns)=="boolean" then setState(ns,fire) else setState(not state,true) end end
    return BoundToggles[text]
end

function makeMainTextBox(parent,text,default,placeholder,callback)
    local row=Instance.new("Frame"); row.Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,31); row.BackgroundColor3=Theme.Panel; row.BackgroundTransparency=0.18; row.Parent=parent; corner(row,6)
    local l=Instance.new("TextLabel"); l.Size=UDim2.new(1,-100,1,0); l.Position=UDim2.new(0,8,0,0); l.BackgroundTransparency=1; l.Text=text; l.TextColor3=Theme.Text; l.Font=Enum.Font.GothamMedium; l.TextSize=(text=="Min Gen for Auto TP" and 14 or 10); l.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; l.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd; l.Parent=row
    local box=Instance.new("TextBox"); box.Size=UDim2.new(0,80,0,21); box.Position=UDim2.new(1,-88,0.5,-10.5); box.BackgroundColor3=Theme.InputBg; box.BorderSizePixel=0; box.Text=default or ""; box.PlaceholderText=placeholder or ""; box.TextColor3=Theme.Text; box.Font=Enum.Font.GothamMedium; box.TextSize=10; box.ClearTextOnFocus=false; box.Parent=row; corner(box,4)
    box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local raw = box.Text:gsub("%s", "")
        if callback then callback(raw) end
    end)
    return box
end

function makeKeybindRow(parent,nameText)
    local row=Instance.new("Frame"); row.Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,31); row.BackgroundColor3=Theme.Panel; row.BackgroundTransparency=0.18; row.Parent=parent; corner(row,6)
    local l=Instance.new("TextLabel"); l.Size=UDim2.new(1,-88,1,0); l.Position=UDim2.new(0,8,0,0); l.BackgroundTransparency=1; l.Text=nameText; l.TextColor3=Theme.Text; l.Font=Enum.Font.GothamSemibold; l.TextSize=10; l.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; l.Parent=row
    local x=Instance.new("TextButton"); x.Name="WhiteTextBtn"; x.Size=UDim2.new(0,22,0,20); x.Position=UDim2.new(1,-74,0.5,-10); x.BackgroundColor3=Theme.Red; x.Text="X"; x.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1); x.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; x.TextSize=10; x.Parent=row; corner(x,5)
    local key=Instance.new("TextButton"); key.Name="WhiteTextBtn"; key.Size=UDim2.new(0,50,0,20); key.Position=UDim2.new(1,-50,0.5,-10); key.BackgroundColor3=Theme.Accent; key.Text=Keybinds[nameText] or "NONE"; key.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1); key.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; key.TextSize=9; key.Parent=row; corner(key,5)
    x.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Keybinds[nameText]="NONE"; Config.keybinds[nameText]="NONE"; saveConfig(); key.Text="NONE"; if updateMovementPanelLabels then updateMovementPanelLabels() end end)
    key.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() key.Text="..."
        local con; con=UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input,gp) if gp then return end; if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then Keybinds[nameText]=input.KeyCode.Name; Config.keybinds[nameText]=input.KeyCode.Name; if nameText=="Carpet Boost" then Config.TpSettings.CarpetSpeedKey=input.KeyCode.Name; Config.keybinds["Carpet Speed"]=nil; Keybinds["Carpet Speed"]=nil end; saveConfig(); key.Text=input.KeyCode.Name; if nameText=="Open Menu" then UI.OpenMenuKey=input.KeyCode end; con:Disconnect(); if updateMovementPanelLabels then updateMovementPanelLabels() end end end)
    end)
end

-- CREATE PANELS
main,mainBody=makeMainPanel("PORK HUB",UDim2.new(0,720,0,620),UDim2.new(0.5,-360,0.5,-310))
if Config.AutoCloseOnExec then main.Visible = false end
-- ===================== MINI ACTIONS (combined panel) =====================
-- Invisible Steal + Steal + Actions merged into ONE window titled "Invis Panel".
-- Only panel *construction* changes here: StealBody / InvisStealBody / ActionsBody
-- now point at section frames INSIDE this one panel, so every row/toggle/callback
-- that populates them further down stays byte-for-byte identical. The old panel
-- keys are kept pointing at this same frame so all existing theme / saved-position /
-- visibility references remain valid (no nil, no logic change).
miniPanel, miniBody = makeQuickPanel("PORK HUB\nInvisible Steal",UDim2.new(0,456,0,520),UDim2.new(1,-472,0,10))
function makeMiniSection(titleText, ord)
    -- Hidden section header: keeps Steal/Invis Steal/Actions sections working,
    -- but removes the extra category text from the Invis Panel panel.
    local sec=Instance.new("Frame"); sec.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0); sec.AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y; sec.BackgroundTransparency=1
    sec.LayoutOrder=ord*10+1; sec.Parent=miniBody
    local lay=Instance.new("UIListLayout"); lay.Padding=UDim.new(0,4); lay.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder; lay.Parent=sec
    return sec
end
panels["Invis Panel"]=miniPanel
panels["StealBody"]=makeMiniSection("STEAL",1)
panels["InvisStealBody"]=makeMiniSection("INVIS STEAL",2)
panels["ActionsBody"]=makeMiniSection("ACTIONS",3)

-- Responsive default layout helper. Saved user positions always win.
function applyDefaultPosition(frame, saveKey, desktopPosition, compactPosition)
    if not frame or (Config.positions and Config.positions[saveKey]) then return end
    local viewport = Workspace.CurrentCamera and Workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1366, 768)
    frame.Position = (viewport.X < 1050 or viewport.Y < 650) and compactPosition or desktopPosition
end

-- Compact Pork Buttons: small old-style panel that mirrors the important Invis Panel controls.
panels["Pork Buttons"], panels["MiniUIBody"] = makeQuickPanel("PORK HUB\nQuick Actions", UDim2.new(0,308,0,396), UDim2.new(0,14,0,60))

-- Live mini window: every enabled feature is listed here and updates instantly.
activeFeaturesPanel, activeFeaturesBody = makeQuickPanel("PORK HUB\nActive Features", UDim2.new(0,286,0,320), UDim2.new(1,-300,0,60))
panels["Active Features"] = activeFeaturesPanel

function setActiveFeaturesVisible(on)
    on = on == true
    Config.Visibilities = Config.Visibilities or {}
    Config.Visibilities["Active Features"] = on
    _G._SXEPanelVis = _G._SXEPanelVis or {}
    _G._SXEPanelVis["Active Features"] = on

    -- Keep both main-window toggle labels synchronized.
    setToggle("Active Features", on)
    setToggle("Active Features Mini UI", on)

    if activeFeaturesPanel then
        if on then
            if rebuildActiveFeatures then rebuildActiveFeatures() end
            openAnim(activeFeaturesPanel)
        else
            closeAnim(activeFeaturesPanel)
        end
    end
    saveConfig()
end
_G.__SXESetActiveFeaturesVisible = setActiveFeaturesVisible

-- Spread the compact Pork Buttons controls out so the panel uses its vertical space better.
pcall(function()
    local miniLay = panels["MiniUIBody"]:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
    if miniLay then miniLay.Padding = UDim.new(0,14) end
    panels["MiniUIBody"].Size = UDim2.new(1,-12,1,-56)
end)

    left.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftButton
    left.Text = leftText
    left.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    left.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    left.TextSize = 12
    left.AutoButtonColor = false
    left.Parent = row
    corner(left,8)
    addPorkHover(left, Theme.SoftButton, Theme.SoftButtonHover, 1.025)
    left.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if leftCallback then leftCallback() end end)
    local right = Instance.new("TextButton")
    right.Size = UDim2.new(0.5,-3,1,0)
    right.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,3,0,0)
    right.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftButton
    right.Text = rightText
    right.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    right.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    right.TextSize = 12
    right.AutoButtonColor = false
    right.Parent = row
    corner(right,8)
    addPorkHover(right, Theme.SoftButton, Theme.SoftButtonHover, 1.025)
    right.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if rightCallback then rightCallback() end end)
    return row
end

function makeMiniPorkToggle(parent, labelText, toggleName, callback)
    regToggle(toggleName, getToggle(toggleName))
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Name = "WhiteTextBtn"
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(1,-4,0,48)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = getToggle(toggleName) and Theme.Green or Theme.SoftButton
    btn.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    btn.TextSize = 12
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.Parent = parent
    corner(btn,8)

    local function refresh(on)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.Green or Theme.SoftButton
        btn.Text = string.upper(labelText) .. "  " .. (on and "ON" or "OFF")
    end

    refresh(getToggle(toggleName))
    addPorkHover(btn, nil, nil, 1.02)
    onToggleChanged(toggleName, refresh)
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local newVal = not getToggle(toggleName)
        setToggle(toggleName, newVal)
        refresh(newVal)
        if callback then callback(newVal) end
    end)
    return btn
end

tpSpeedSettingsPanel.Visible=false
for _,pair in ipairs({{"PORK HUB",main},{"PORK HUB\nInvisible Steal",panels["Invis Panel"]},{"PORK HUB\nQuick Actions",panels["Pork Buttons"]},

    {"PORK HUB\nActive Features",activeFeaturesPanel},
    {"PORK HUB\nAction Settings",actionSettingsPanel},{"PORK HUB\nTP & Clone Settings",tpSpeedSettingsPanel}}) do applySavedPosition(pair[1],pair[2]) end
task.defer(function() if _G.PorkHubFitAllPanels then _G.PorkHubFitAllPanels() end end)

-- ROBUST PANEL POSITION PERSISTENCE
-- The shared drag/resize save chain was unreliable for some panels (Steal Target
-- especially), so a dragged panel kept reopening at its default spot after a
-- rejoin / new lobby. This is a self-contained safety net that does NOT depend on
-- makeDraggable: it re-applies each panel's saved position once the UI has fully
-- settled, then autosaves whenever the user actually moves the panel (debounced),
-- so panels always reopen exactly where they were left.
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(1.2)
    local watched = {
        {"PORK HUB\nSteal Target", panels["Steal Target"]},
        {"PORK HUB\nInvisible Steal", panels["Invis Panel"]},
        {"PORK HUB\nQuick Actions", panels["Pork Buttons"]},
        {"PORK HUB\nCommand Cooldowns", panels["Command Cooldowns"]},
        {"PORK HUB\nActive Features", activeFeaturesPanel},
    }
    for _, pair in ipairs(watched) do
        local key, frame = pair[1], pair[2]
        if frame then
            -- Re-apply the saved position AFTER build/refresh/fit have run, so any
            -- step that nudged it back to default is corrected. Connect the watcher
            -- only after this, so the re-apply itself never triggers a save.
            if Config.positions and Config.positions[key] then
                applySavedPosition(key, frame)
            end
            local token = 0
            frame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"):Connect(function()
                token = token + 1
                local myToken = token
                task.delay(0.4, function()
                    -- Save only after movement settles and only if this is still the
                    -- latest change, so we persist the final resting position.
                    if myToken == token and frame and frame.Parent and not UI.Locked then
                        rememberPosition(key, frame)
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end
    end
end)

-- LAZY UI LOADING
if _G.addLazyUI then
    _G.addLazyUI(main, not Config.AutoCloseOnExec)
end
-- Floating settings windows are created immediately. Keeping them out of the
-- delayed lazy queue prevents that queue from hiding a window after the user opens it.
actionSettingsPanel.Visible = Config.CompactPopups.ActionSettings == true
tpSpeedSettingsPanel.Visible = Config.CompactPopups.TPSettings == true

immediatePanels = {
    ["Steal Target"] = true,
    ["Invis Panel"] = true,
    ["Pork Buttons"] = true,
    ["Active Features"] = true
}

for name, panel in pairs(panels) do
    if not string.match(name, "Body$") then
        -- _G._SXEPanelVis survives re-executes; override Config if it has a value
        local fromG = _G._SXEPanelVis[name]
        local targetVis
        if fromG ~= nil then
            targetVis = fromG
            Config.Visibilities[name] = fromG  -- sync back so Config is consistent
        else
            if Config.Visibilities[name] ~= nil then targetVis = Config.Visibilities[name] else targetVis = true end
        end
        if immediatePanels[name] then
            panel.Visible = targetVis
        elseif _G.addLazyUI then
            _G.addLazyUI(panel, targetVis, false, name)
        end
    end
end

-- ACTIVE FEATURES MINI PANEL
local ACTIVE_FEATURE_NAME_ALIASES = {
    ["XRay"] = "X-Ray", ["Xray"] = "X-Ray",
    ["DarkMode"] = "Dark Mode",
    ["ClickToAP"] = "Click to AP",
    ["ClickToAPSingle"] = "Click AP Single Cmd",
    ["AntiBeeDisco"] = "Anti-Bee & Anti-Disco",
    ["Carpet Speed"] = "Carpet Boost",
}
local ACTIVE_FEATURE_HIDDEN = {
    ["Dark Mode"] = true,
    ["Invis Panel"] = true,
    ["Pork Buttons"] = true,
    ["Command Cooldowns"] = true,
    ["Steal Target"] = true,
    ["Active Features"] = true,
    ["Active Features Mini UI"] = true,
    ["TP Speed Window"] = true,
    ["Auto Close Main UI on Execute"] = true,
    ["Instant Clone"] = true,
}

function rebuildActiveFeatures()
    if not activeFeaturesBody or not activeFeaturesBody.Parent then return end
    clearBody(activeFeaturesBody)

    local enabledNames = {}
    local seenNames = {}
    local function addEnabled(rawName)
        local displayName = ACTIVE_FEATURE_NAME_ALIASES[rawName] or rawName
        if not ACTIVE_FEATURE_HIDDEN[displayName] and not seenNames[displayName] then
            seenNames[displayName] = true
            table.insert(enabledNames, displayName)
        end
    end

    for rawName, data in pairs(ToggleState) do
        if data and data.value == true then addEnabled(rawName) end
    end

    -- Include saved features that may not have built their tab yet. This makes
    -- the mini UI complete immediately after execution instead of only after the
    -- user visits every page in the main window.
    local savedFeatureStates = {
        ["Auto Unlock During Steal"] = Config.AutoUnlockOnSteal,
        ["Auto Recover Lagback"] = Config.AutoRecoverLagback,
        ["Show TP Path"] = Config.ShowTpPath,
        ["Line To Base"] = Config.LineToBase,
        ["Line To Best Brainrot"] = Config.LineToBrainrot,
        ["TP to Carpet Brainrots"] = Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.TpToConveyor,
        ["Kick to Private Server"] = Config.KickToPrivateServer,
        ["Priority Sound Alert"] = Config.PrioritySoundAlert,
        ["Clear Error Popups"] = Config.CleanErrorGUIs,
    }
    for featureName, isOn in pairs(savedFeatureStates) do
        if isOn == true then addEnabled(featureName) end
    end

    table.sort(enabledNames, function(a,b) return string.lower(a) < string.lower(b) end)

    local countLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    countLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1,-4,0,22)
    countLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    countLabel.Text = tostring(#enabledNames) .. " ENABLED"
    countLabel.TextColor3 = #enabledNames > 0 and Theme.AccentLight or Theme.Dim
    countLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    countLabel.TextSize = 10
    countLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    countLabel.Parent = activeFeaturesBody

    if #enabledNames == 0 then
        local empty = card(activeFeaturesBody, 42)
        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1,-20,1,0)
        label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10,0)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Text = "No features are enabled"
        label.TextColor3 = Theme.Dim
        label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        label.TextSize = 11
        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        label.Parent = empty
    else
        for _, featureName in ipairs(enabledNames) do
            local row = card(activeFeaturesBody, 34)

            local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
            dot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(9,9)
            dot.Position = UDim2.new(0,12,0.5,-4)
            dot.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Green
            dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
            dot.Parent = row
            corner(dot, 20)

            local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            label.Size = UDim2.new(1,-38,1,0)
            label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(30,0)
            label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            label.Text = featureName
            label.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
            label.TextSize = 11
            label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            label.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            label.Parent = row
        end
    end

    makeQuickButton(activeFeaturesBody, "Turn Active Features UI OFF", function()
        setActiveFeaturesVisible(false)
    end, Theme.SoftAccent)

    if _G.applySXEUITransparency then
        pcall(_G.applySXEUITransparency, Config.UITransparency or 0.12)
    end
end
_G.__SXERefreshActiveFeatures = rebuildActiveFeatures

-- ACTIONS PANEL 
function rebuildActions()
    clearBody(panels["ActionsBody"])
    -- Invis Panel quick buttons removed by request:
    -- Ragdoll Self, Rejoin PS, Rejoin Job ID, Kick, Kick To Private, Reset, Settings.
    -- Optional utility toggles can still appear here if enabled from config.
    if actionConfig["Anti Ragdoll"] then makeSyncStateRow(panels["ActionsBody"],"Anti Ragdoll:","Anti Ragdoll",function(on) if on then startAntiRagdoll() else stopAntiRagdoll() end end) end
    if actionConfig["Infinite Jump"] then makeSyncStateRow(panels["ActionsBody"],"Infinite Jump:","Infinite Jump",function(on) if _G.setInfiniteJump then _G.setInfiniteJump(on) end end) end
    if actionConfig["Float"] then makeSyncStateRow(panels["ActionsBody"],"Float:","Float",function(on) if _G.setFloat then _G.setFloat(on) end end) end
    if actionConfig["Carpet Speed"] then makeSyncStateRow(panels["ActionsBody"],"Carpet Speed:","Carpet Speed",function(on) if _G.setCarpetSpeed then _G.setCarpetSpeed(on) end end) end
end

function rebuildActionSettings()
    clearBody(actionSettingsBody)
    for actionName,enabled in pairs(actionConfig) do
        local row=Instance.new("Frame"); row.Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,34); row.BackgroundTransparency=1; row.Parent=actionSettingsBody
        local label=Instance.new("TextLabel"); label.Size=UDim2.new(1,-84,1,0); label.Position=UDim2.new(0,4,0,0); label.BackgroundTransparency=1; label.Text=actionName; label.TextColor3=Theme.Text; label.Font=Enum.Font.GothamSemibold; label.TextSize=11; label.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; label.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd; label.Parent=row
        local btn=Instance.new("TextButton"); btn.Name="WhiteTextBtn"; btn.Size=UDim2.new(0,72,0,30); btn.Position=UDim2.new(1,-74,0.5,-15); btn.BackgroundColor3=enabled and Theme.Green or Theme.ToggleOff2; btn.Text=enabled and "ON" or "OFF"; btn.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1); btn.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; btn.TextSize=12; btn.AutoButtonColor=false; btn.Parent=row; corner(btn,6)
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            actionConfig[actionName]=not actionConfig[actionName]; Config.actions[actionName]=actionConfig[actionName]; saveConfig()
            btn.BackgroundColor3=actionConfig[actionName] and Theme.Green or Theme.ToggleOff2; btn.Text=actionConfig[actionName] and "ON" or "OFF"
            rebuildActions()
        end)
    end
    makeQuickButton(actionSettingsBody,"Close",function()
        if _G.__PorkHubSetPopupState then
            _G.__PorkHubSetPopupState("ActionSettings", false)
        else
            Config.CompactPopups = Config.CompactPopups or {}
            Config.CompactPopups.ActionSettings = false
            closeAnim(actionSettingsPanel)
            saveConfig()
        end
    end,Theme.SoftAccentHover)
end

function rebuildTpSpeedSettings()
    clearBody(tpSpeedSettingsBody)
    makeMainSliderWithInput(tpSpeedSettingsBody, "100 Studs Base Speed", 20, 250, Config.TpSettings.FlyTPCloseSpeed or 75, function(v) Config.TpSettings.FlyTPCloseSpeed=v; saveConfig() end)
    makeMainSliderWithInput(tpSpeedSettingsBody, "Carpet / Grabble Speed", 50, 600, math.clamp(tonumber(Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed) or 600, 50, 600), function(v) v=math.clamp(tonumber(v) or 600,50,600); Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed=v; saveConfig(); if _G.SXESetCarpetSpeed then pcall(_G.SXESetCarpetSpeed, v) end end)
    -- "Walk To Brainrot Speed" slider removed: the go-to-brainrot approach is now a fixed 180.
    makeMainSliderWithInput(tpSpeedSettingsBody, "Clone Delay", 0.05, 2.0, Config.TpSettings.CloneDelayVal or 0.1, function(v) Config.TpSettings.CloneDelayVal=v; saveConfig() end, "s")
    makeQuickButton(tpSpeedSettingsBody, "Close", function()
        if _G.__PorkHubSetPopupState then
            _G.__PorkHubSetPopupState("TPSettings", false)
        else
            Config.CompactPopups = Config.CompactPopups or {}
            Config.CompactPopups.TPSettings = false
            closeAnim(tpSpeedSettingsPanel)
            saveConfig()
        end
    end, Theme.SoftAccentHover)
end

_G.__PorkHubSetPopupState = function(popupName, visible)
    Config.CompactPopups = Config.CompactPopups or {}
    visible = visible and true or false
    Config.CompactPopups[popupName] = visible

    local target
    if popupName == "TPSettings" then
        target = tpSpeedSettingsPanel
        setToggle("TP Speed Window", visible)
        if visible then rebuildTpSpeedSettings() end
    elseif popupName == "ActionSettings" then
        target = actionSettingsPanel
    end

    if target then
        if _G.cancelLazyUI then pcall(_G.cancelLazyUI, target) end
        if visible then openAnim(target) else closeAnim(target) end
    end
    saveConfig()
end

do
    -- INVISIBLE STEAL PANEL POPULATION (Pork neon redesign, scoped to this panel only)

        end
        body.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
        body.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 468)
        body.BackgroundTransparency = 1

        local porkPink = Theme.Accent
        local porkPink2 = Theme.AccentLight
        local darkCard = Theme.Panel
        local darkCard2 = Theme.Row
        local darkOff = Theme.ToggleOff
        local dimText = Theme.Dim

            c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 12)
            c.Parent = obj
            return c
        end

        local function addStroke(obj, col, thick, trans)
            return nil
        end

        local function makeIcon(parent, text, pos, size, active)
            local box = Instance.new("Frame")

            box.Parent = parent
            addCorner(box, 13)
            addStroke(box, active and porkPink or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 78), 1, active and 0.08 or 0.55)

            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            lbl.TextSize = active and 16 or 14

        local function makeCard(parent, pos, size, title, subtitle)
