            grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, darkCard2),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, darkCard),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(7, 8, 12))
            })
            grad.Parent = card

            if title and title ~= "" then
                local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                t.Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 24)
                t.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 12)
                t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                t.Text = title
                t.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
                t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                t.TextSize = 14
                t.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                t.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                t.Parent = card
            end
            if subtitle and subtitle ~= "" then
                local sub = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 18)
                sub.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 36)
                sub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                sub.Text = subtitle
                sub.TextColor3 = dimText
                sub.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
                sub.TextSize = 10
                sub.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                sub.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                sub.Parent = card
            end
            return card
        end

        local function makePillToggle(parent, toggleName, callback, pos)
            regToggle(toggleName, getToggle(toggleName))
            local pill = Instance.new("TextButton")
            pill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 86, 0, 30)
            pill.Position = pos or UDim2.new(1, -106, 0, 16)
            pill.BackgroundColor3 = getToggle(toggleName) and porkPink or darkOff
            pill.Text = getToggle(toggleName) and "ON" or "OFF"
            pill.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            pill.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            pill.TextSize = 12
            pill.AutoButtonColor = false
            pill.Parent = parent
            addCorner(pill, 15)
            addStroke(pill, getToggle(toggleName) and porkPink2 or Color3.fromRGB(115, 115, 125), 1, 0.15)

            local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
            knob.Name = "WhiteSliderKnob"
            knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
            knob.Position = getToggle(toggleName) and UDim2.new(1, -26, 0.5, -11) or UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -11)
            knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
            knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
            knob.Parent = pill
            addCorner(knob, 11)

            local function refresh(on)
                pill.BackgroundColor3 = on and porkPink or darkOff
                pill.Text = on and "ON" or "OFF"
                knob:TweenPosition(on and UDim2.new(1, -26, 0.5, -11) or UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -11), Enum.EasingDirection.Out, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, 0.14, true)
            end
            onToggleChanged(toggleName, refresh)
            pill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local newVal = not getToggle(toggleName)
                setToggle(toggleName, newVal)
                refresh(newVal)
                if callback then callback(newVal) end
            end)
            refresh(getToggle(toggleName))
            return pill
        end

        local function makeNeonSlider(parent, name, minv, maxv, current, callback, pos, size, circular)
            local box = makeCard(parent, pos, size, name, nil)

            local valueBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
            valueBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 62, 0, 24)
            valueBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -74, 0, 10)
            valueBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(9, 10, 14)
            valueBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04
            valueBox.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            valueBox.PlaceholderColor3 = dimText
            valueBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            valueBox.TextSize = 11
            valueBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
            valueBox.TextEditable = true
            valueBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            valueBox.Text = circular and (tostring(math.floor(current * 10 + 0.5) / 10) .. "°") or tostring(math.floor(current * 10 + 0.5) / 10)
            valueBox.Parent = box
            addCorner(valueBox, 8)
            addStroke(valueBox, porkPink, 1, 0.35)

            local track = Instance.new("Frame")
            track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0, 9)
            track.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 1, -38)
            track.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(48, 48, 56)
            track.BorderSizePixel = 0
            track.Parent = box
            addCorner(track, 6)

            local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
            fill.BackgroundColor3 = porkPink
            fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
            fill.Parent = track
            addCorner(fill, 6)
            local fg = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            fg.Rotation = 0
            fg.Color = ColorSequence.new(porkPink, porkPink2)
            fg.Parent = fill

            local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
            knob.Name = "WhiteSliderKnob"
            knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
            knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
            knob.Parent = track
            addCorner(knob, 11)
            addStroke(knob, porkPink2, 1, 0.15)

            local leftNum = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            leftNum.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 16)
            leftNum.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 1, -22)
            leftNum.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            leftNum.Text = tostring(minv)
            leftNum.TextColor3 = dimText
            leftNum.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            leftNum.TextSize = 10
            leftNum.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            leftNum.Parent = box

            local rightNum = leftNum:Clone()
            rightNum.Position = UDim2.new(1, -56, 1, -22)
            rightNum.Text = tostring(maxv)
            rightNum.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
            rightNum.Parent = box

            local val = math.clamp(tonumber(current) or minv, minv, maxv)
            local dragging = false
            local function setVal(v, fire)
                val = math.clamp(v, minv, maxv)
                local pct = (val - minv) / (maxv - minv)
                fill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
                knob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, -11, 0.5, -11)
                local shown = math.floor(val * 10 + 0.5) / 10
                valueBox.Text = circular and (tostring(shown) .. "°") or tostring(shown)
                if fire and callback then callback(shown) end
            end
            local function fromX(x)
                local rel = (x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1)
                setVal(minv + (maxv - minv) * rel, true)
            end
            local function applyTypedValue()
                local raw = tostring(valueBox.Text or ""):gsub("°", ""):gsub("[^%d%.-]", "")
                local typed = tonumber(raw)
                if typed then
                    setVal(typed, true)
                else
                    setVal(val, false)
                end
            end
            valueBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
                applyTypedValue()
            end)
            valueBox.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Return or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadEnter then
                    valueBox:ReleaseFocus()
                end
            end)
            track.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging = true
                    fromX(input.Position.X)
                end
            end)
            track.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging = false end
            end)
            UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    fromX(input.Position.X)
                end
            end)
            setVal(val, false)
            return box
        end

        local root = Instance.new("Frame")
        root.Name = "PorkInvisRedesignOnly"
        -- PATCH: force compact invis panel final sizing / no clipped card corners
        pcall(function()
            miniPanel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 456, 0, 520)
            -- Only fall back to the default position if the user hasn't dragged/saved
            -- one. This was unconditionally overwriting the saved position every load,
            -- which is why the Invis Panel position didn't persist on rejoin / new lobbies.
            if Config.positions and Config.positions["PORK HUB\nInvisible Steal"] then
                applySavedPosition("PORK HUB\nInvisible Steal", miniPanel)
            else
                miniPanel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -528, 0.5, -270)
            end
            miniPanel.ClipsDescendants = false
            miniBody.ClipsDescendants = false
            miniBody.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
            miniBody.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 520)
            miniBody.ScrollBarThickness = 3
        end)
        -- PATCH: Invis Panel compact/smoother layout applied
        root.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 496)
        root.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        root.LayoutOrder = 1
        root.Parent = body

        -- Clean Invis Panel layout: no left icon strip and no emojis/icons.
        local hero = makeCard(root, UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0), UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 64), "INVISIBILITY", "Make yourself invisible from everyone.")
        makePillToggle(hero, "Invisible Steal", function(on)
            if _G.toggleInvisibleSteal then pcall(_G.toggleInvisibleSteal) end
        end, UDim2.new(1, -98, 0, 17))

        makeNeonSlider(root, "ROTATION", 0, 360, Config.InvisStealAngle or 225, function(v)
            _G.InvisStealAngle = v
            Config.InvisStealAngle = v
            saveConfig()
        end, UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 76), UDim2.new(0.5, -14, 0, 104), true)

        makeNeonSlider(root, "DEPTH", 0, 18, Config.SinkSliderValue or 7, function(v)
            _G.SinkSliderValue = v
            Config.SinkSliderValue = v
            saveConfig()
        end, UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 76), UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0, 104), false)

        local autoCard = makeCard(root, UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 198), UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 120), "", "")
        local function autoLine(y, label, sub, toggleName, cb)
            local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            l.Size = UDim2.new(1, -122, 0, 18)
            l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, y)
            l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            l.Text = label
            l.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            l.TextSize = 12
            l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            l.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            l.Parent = autoCard
            if sub and sub ~= "" then
                local s = l:Clone()
                s.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, y + 18)
                s.Text = sub
                s.TextColor3 = dimText
                s.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
                s.TextSize = 9
                s.Parent = autoCard
            end
            makePillToggle(autoCard, toggleName, cb, UDim2.new(1, -96, 0, y - 5))
        end
        autoLine(40, "AUTO RECOVER", "", "Auto Recover Lagback", function(on)
            _G.AutoRecoverLagback = on
            Config.AutoRecoverLagback = on
            saveConfig()
        end)
        autoLine(74, "AUTO INVIS", "", "Auto Invis During Steal", function(on)
            _G.AutoInvisDuringSteal = on
            Config.AutoInvisDuringSteal = on
            saveConfig()
        end)

        local speedCard = makeCard(root, UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 332), UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 132), "WALK SPEED", "Movement stays inside the panel now.")
        makePillToggle(speedCard, "WalkSpeed", function(on) setWalkSpeedEnabled(on) end, UDim2.new(1, -98, 0, 12))

        local speedValue = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        speedValue.Size = UDim2.new(0, 62, 0, 24)
        speedValue.Position = UDim2.new(1, -76, 0, 42)
        speedValue.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(9, 10, 14)
        speedValue.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04
        speedValue.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        speedValue.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        speedValue.TextSize = 12
        speedValue.Parent = speedCard
        addCorner(speedValue, 8)
        addStroke(speedValue, porkPink, 1, 0.35)

        local speedTrack = Instance.new("Frame")
        speedTrack.Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0, 9)
        speedTrack.Position = UDim2.new(0, 17, 0, 88)
        speedTrack.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(48, 48, 56)
        speedTrack.BorderSizePixel = 0
        speedTrack.Parent = speedCard
        addCorner(speedTrack, 6)

        local speedFill = Instance.new("Frame")
        speedFill.BackgroundColor3 = porkPink
        speedFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        speedFill.Parent = speedTrack
        addCorner(speedFill, 6)

        local speedKnob = Instance.new("Frame")
        speedKnob.Name = "WhiteSliderKnob"
        speedKnob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
        speedKnob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        speedKnob.BorderSizePixel = 0
        speedKnob.Parent = speedTrack
        addCorner(speedKnob, 11)
        addStroke(speedKnob, porkPink2, 1, 0.15)

        local speedMin = 15
        local speedMax = 29
        local speedVal = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.WalkSpeedValue) or 16, speedMin, speedMax)
        local speedDrag = false
        local function setSpeedVisual(v, fire)
            speedVal = math.clamp(v, speedMin, speedMax)
            local pct = (speedVal - speedMin) / (speedMax - speedMin)
            speedFill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
            speedKnob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, -11, 0.5, -11)
            local shown = math.floor(speedVal * 10 + 0.5) / 10
            speedValue.Text = tostring(shown)
            if fire then setWalkSpeedValue(shown) end
        end
        local function setSpeedFromX(x)
            local rel = (x - speedTrack.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(speedTrack.AbsoluteSize.X, 1)
            setSpeedVisual(speedMin + (speedMax - speedMin) * rel, true)
        end
        speedTrack.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                speedDrag = true
                setSpeedFromX(input.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        speedTrack.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                speedDrag = false
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if speedDrag and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                setSpeedFromX(input.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        setSpeedVisual(speedVal, false)

        _G.updateMovementPanelLabels = function()
            pcall(function()
                if panels and panels["ActionsBody"] and rebuildActions then rebuildActions() end
                if UI and (UI.CurrentTab == "Keybind" or UI.CurrentTab == "Keybinds") and loadTab then
                    task.defer(function() loadTab("Keybinds") end)
                end
            end)
        end
        if Config.WalkSpeedEnabled then
            task.defer(function() setWalkSpeedEnabled(true) end)
        end
        task.defer(function()
            if miniBody and miniBody.Parent then
                miniBody.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 520)
            end
            if _G.PorkHubFitAllPanels then
                pcall(_G.PorkHubFitAllPanels)
            end
        end)
    end


-- ADMIN QUICK PANEL
function spamPlayerBaseOwner(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer then return 0 end
    local cmdList = Config.SpamBaseOwnerOrder or AP_ALL_COMMANDS
    
    if Config.SpamBaseOwnerSingleCommand then
        local startIndex = _G.SpamBaseOwnerIndex or 1
        if startIndex > #cmdList then startIndex = 1 end
        
        local picked = nil
        local attempts = 0
        while attempts < #cmdList do
            local idx = ((startIndex - 1 + attempts) % #cmdList) + 1
            local cmd = cmdList[idx]
            if Config.SpamBaseOwnerCommands and Config.SpamBaseOwnerCommands[cmd] and not apIsOnCooldown(cmd) then
                picked = cmd
                _G.SpamBaseOwnerIndex = idx + 1
                break
            end
            attempts = attempts + 1
        end
        
        if picked then
            runAdminCommand(targetPlayer, picked)
            return 1
        end
        return 0
    else
        local activeCmds = {}
        for _, cmd in ipairs(cmdList) do
            if Config.SpamBaseOwnerCommands and Config.SpamBaseOwnerCommands[cmd] and not apIsOnCooldown(cmd) then
                table.insert(activeCmds, cmd)
            end
        end
        for i, cmd in ipairs(activeCmds) do
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait((i - 1) * 0.01)
                runAdminCommand(targetPlayer, cmd)
            end)
        end
        return #activeCmds
    end
end

-- ADMIN QUICK PANEL
function spamBaseOwner()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then
        ShowNotification("SPAM OWNER", "No character found")
        return
    end
    local nearestPlot = nil
    local nearestDist = math.huge
    local Plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if Plots then
        for _, plot in ipairs(Plots:GetChildren()) do
            local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            if sign then
                local yourBase = sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
                if not yourBase or not yourBase.Enabled then
                    local signPos = (sign:IsA("BasePart") and sign.Position)
                        or (sign.PrimaryPart and sign.PrimaryPart.Position)
                    if not signPos then
                        local part = sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                        signPos = part and part.Position
                    end
                    if signPos then
                        local dist = (hrp.Position - signPos).Magnitude
                        if dist < nearestDist then
                            nearestDist = dist
                            nearestPlot = plot
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if not nearestPlot then
        ShowNotification("SPAM OWNER", "No nearby base found")
        return
    end
    local targetPlayer = nil
    local ok, Synchronizer = pcall(require, ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages"):WaitForChild("Synchronizer"))
    if ok and Synchronizer then
        local ch = Synchronizer:Get(nearestPlot.Name)
        if ch then
            local owner = ch:Get("Owner")
            if owner then
                if (typeof(owner) == "Instance") and owner:IsA("Player") then
                    targetPlayer = owner
                elseif (type(owner) == "table") and owner.Name then
                    targetPlayer = Players:FindFirstChild(owner.Name)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if not targetPlayer then
        local sign = nearestPlot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
        local textLabel = sign
            and sign:FindFirstChild("SurfaceGui")
            and sign.SurfaceGui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
            and sign.SurfaceGui.Frame:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
        if textLabel then
            local baseText = textLabel.Text
            local nickname = (baseText and baseText:match("^(.-)'")) or baseText
            if nickname then
                for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if (p.DisplayName == nickname) or (p.Name == nickname) then
                        targetPlayer = p
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if not targetPlayer or (targetPlayer == LocalPlayer) then
        ShowNotification("SPAM OWNER", "Owner not found or is you")
        return
    end
    if isPlayerBlacklisted(targetPlayer) then
        ShowNotification("SPAM OWNER", targetPlayer.DisplayName .. " is blacklisted")
        return
    end
    ShowNotification("SPAM OWNER", "Spamming " .. targetPlayer.DisplayName)
    local sentCount = spamPlayerBaseOwner(targetPlayer)
    ShowNotification("SPAM OWNER", "Sent " .. tostring(sentCount) .. " commands")
end

-- COOLDOWN PANEL
LazyInit("Cooldown Panel", function() -- COOLDOWN PANEL SCOPE
cooldownLabels={}
for _,item in ipairs({"jail","rocket","inverse","ragdoll","jumpscare","tiny","balloon","morph","nightvision"}) do
    local row=Instance.new("Frame"); row.Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,24); row.BackgroundTransparency=1; row.Parent=panels["CooldownBody"]
    local left=Instance.new("TextLabel"); left.Size=UDim2.new(0.58,0,1,0); left.Position=UDim2.new(0,6,0,0); left.BackgroundTransparency=1; left.Text=item:sub(1,1):upper()..item:sub(2); left.TextColor3=Theme.Text; left.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; left.TextSize=12; left.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; left.Parent=row
    local right=Instance.new("TextLabel"); right.Size=UDim2.new(0.36,0,1,0); right.Position=UDim2.new(0.62,0,0,0); right.BackgroundTransparency=1; right.Text="READY"; right.TextColor3=Theme.Green; right.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; right.TextSize=11; right.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Right; right.Parent=row
    cooldownLabels[item]=right
end
task.spawn(function() while true do task.wait(0.5); for cmd,label in pairs(cooldownLabels) do local rem=apGetRemaining(cmd); if rem>0 then label.Text=string.format("%.0fs",rem); label.TextColor3=Theme.Red else label.Text="READY"; label.TextColor3=Theme.Green end end end end)
end) -- END COOLDOWN PANEL SCOPE (LazyInit)

-- STEAL TARGET (0.4s refresh) -- NOT lazy: panel must populate on load
-- Restyled to match the compact black Auto Steal list layout while keeping the Pork/pink theme.
do -- STEAL TARGET SCOPE
function refreshTargetPanel()
    clearBody(panels["TargetBody"])

    -- Make this panel feel like the black reference: compact list, thin rows, footer tabs.
    pcall(function()
        panels["Steal Target"].Size = UDim2.new(0, 390, 0, 335)
        -- Match the Invis Panel exactly so Steal Target is not darker.
        -- Use the same base colour/transparency before the global UI transparency slider is applied.
        local invisPanel = panels["Invis Panel"]
        if invisPanel then
            panels["Steal Target"].BackgroundColor3 = invisPanel.BackgroundColor3
            panels["Steal Target"].BackgroundTransparency = invisPanel.BackgroundTransparency
        else
            panels["Steal Target"].BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Background
            panels["Steal Target"].BackgroundTransparency = 0.04
        end
        panels["TargetBody"].Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 58)
        -- Keep the target list scrollable by itself, with room for exactly 5 compact rows.
        -- The mode buttons + Auto Steal button stay fixed at the bottom of the smaller panel.
        panels["TargetBody"].Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 175)
        panels["TargetBody"].ScrollBarThickness = 2
        panels["TargetBody"].ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Accent
        local lay = panels["TargetBody"]:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
        if lay then lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 0) end
        if _G.applySXEUITransparency then
            task.defer(function()
                pcall(_G.applySXEUITransparency, Config and Config.UITransparency or 0.2)
            end)
        end
    end)

    local function makeThinSeparator(parent, trans)
        local sep = Instance.new("Frame")
        if sep then sep.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 1) end
        if sep then sep.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.AccentLight end
        if sep then sep.BackgroundTransparency = trans or 0.78 end
        if sep then sep.BorderSizePixel = 0 end
        if sep then sep.Parent = parent end
        return sep
    end

    local function makeModeFooter()
        local oldFooter = panels["Steal Target"]:FindFirstChild("StealTargetFixedFooter")
        if oldFooter then oldFooter:Destroy() end

        local footer = Instance.new("Frame")
        footer.Name = "StealTargetFixedFooter"
        footer.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 82)
        footer.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -88)
        -- Let the fixed footer inherit the Steal Target panel transparency.
        -- This removes the solid black block behind the mode buttons/Auto Steal button.
        local invisPanel = panels["Invis Panel"]
        footer.BackgroundColor3 = (panels["Steal Target"] and panels["Steal Target"].BackgroundColor3) or ((invisPanel and invisPanel.BackgroundColor3) or Theme.Background)
        footer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        footer.BorderSizePixel = 0
        footer.ZIndex = 20
        footer.Parent = panels["Steal Target"]

        local topSep = Instance.new("Frame")
        topSep.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
        topSep.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        topSep.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.AccentLight
        topSep.BackgroundTransparency = 0.42
        topSep.BorderSizePixel = 0
        topSep.ZIndex = 21
        topSep.Parent = footer

        local modes = {
            {"Nearest", "Nearest", Config.StealNearest},
            {"Highest Gen", "Highest", Config.StealHighest},
            {"Priority", "Priority", Config.StealPriority},
        }
        for i, info in ipairs(modes) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1/3, -4, 0, 34)
            b.Position = UDim2.new((i-1)/3, 2, 0, 8)
            b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            b.Text = info[1]
            b.TextColor3 = info[3] and Theme.Text or Theme.Dim
            b.Font = info[3] and Enum.Font.GothamBlack or Enum.Font.GothamBold
            b.TextSize = 10
            b.ZIndex = 22
            b.AutoButtonColor = false
            b.Parent = footer
            local underline = Instance.new("Frame")
            underline.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 2)
            underline.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 1, -2)
            underline.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
            underline.BackgroundTransparency = info[3] and 0 or 1
            underline.BorderSizePixel = 0
            underline.ZIndex = 23
            underline.Parent = b
            corner(underline, 2)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if setStealMode then setStealMode(info[2]) end
                refreshTargetPanel()
            end)
        end

        local autoBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        autoBtn.Name = "AutoStealFooterBtn"
        autoBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 30)
        autoBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 47)
        autoBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Config.AutoStealEnabled and Theme.Green or Theme.ToggleOff
        autoBtn.Text = Config.AutoStealEnabled and "AUTO STEAL: ON" or "AUTO STEAL: OFF"
        -- Readable on both the black/cyan UI and the Pork pink UI.
        autoBtn.TextColor3 = Config.AutoStealEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(8, 12, 15) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        autoBtn.TextStrokeColor3 = Config.AutoStealEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        autoBtn.TextStrokeTransparency = Config.AutoStealEnabled and 0.82 or 0.35
        autoBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        autoBtn.TextSize = 12
        autoBtn.TextTransparency = 0
        autoBtn.ZIndex = 22
        autoBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        autoBtn.Parent = footer
        corner(autoBtn, 8)
        local autoStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        autoStroke.Color = Config.AutoStealEnabled and Theme.Green or Theme.AccentLight
        autoStroke.Thickness = Config.AutoStealEnabled and 0 or 1
        autoStroke.Transparency = Config.AutoStealEnabled and 1 or 0.38
        autoStroke.Parent = autoBtn
        autoBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local on = not Config.AutoStealEnabled
            autoStealEnabled = on
            Config.AutoStealEnabled = on
            setToggle("Auto Steal", on)
            saveConfig()
            if _G.SXEAutoSteal then pcall(_G.SXEAutoSteal, on) end
            refreshTargetPanel()
        end)
    end

    local cache = get_all_pets()
    if not cache or #cache == 0 then
        for i = 1, 10 do
            local row = Instance.new("Frame")
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 32)
            row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            row.Parent = panels["TargetBody"]

            local n = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            n.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 1, 0)
            n.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
            n.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            n.Text = string.format("%02d", i)
            n.TextColor3 = Theme.Dim
            n.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            n.TextSize = 9
            n.Parent = row

            local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            l.Size = UDim2.new(1, -42, 1, 0)
            l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 0)
            l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            l.Text = "Scanning for Brainrot"
            l.TextColor3 = Theme.Dim
            l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            l.TextSize = 11
            l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            l.Parent = row

            -- Clean Steal Target scan animation: no trailing/random dots.
            -- Keeps the label text stable and uses a soft pulse instead.
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait((i - 1) * 0.07)
                while l and l.Parent and row and row.Parent do
                    l.Text = "Scanning for Brainrot"
                    local pulse = (math.sin(tick() * 4 + i * 0.55) + 1) * 0.5
                    l.TextTransparency = 0.08 + (pulse * 0.18)
                    task.wait(0.08)
                end
            end)

            makeThinSeparator(panels["TargetBody"])
        end
        makeModeFooter()
        return
    end

    -- Build priority lookup
    local prioSet = {}
    for _, pName in ipairs(priorityList) do prioSet[pName:lower()] = true end

    -- Split into priority and non-priority
    local prioPets, otherPets = {}, {}
    for _, pet in ipairs(cache) do
        local isBrainrot = (pet.mpsValue and pet.mpsValue >= 10000000)
        if isBrainrot or (pet.petName and prioSet[pet.petName:lower()]) then
            table.insert(prioPets, pet)
        else
            table.insert(otherPets, pet)
        end
    end

    -- Sort priority pets by priority list order, keeping Brainrots at the absolute top
    table.sort(prioPets, function(a, b)
        local ai, bi = 999, 999
        for idx, pn in ipairs(priorityList) do
            local pnL = pn:lower()
            if a.petName and a.petName:lower() == pnL then ai = idx end
            if b.petName and b.petName:lower() == pnL then bi = idx end
        end
        local aIsBrainrot = (a.mpsValue and a.mpsValue >= 10000000)
        local bIsBrainrot = (b.mpsValue and b.mpsValue >= 10000000)
        if aIsBrainrot and not bIsBrainrot then return true end
        if bIsBrainrot and not aIsBrainrot then return false end
        if ai == bi then return (a.mpsValue or 0) > (b.mpsValue or 0) end
        return ai < bi
    end)

    local function makeTargetRow(pet, displayIndex, isPriority)
        local isSelected = (selectedTargetUID == pet.uid)
        local isManual = (manuallySelectedUID == pet.uid)

        local row = Instance.new("Frame")
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 40)
        row.BackgroundColor3 = isSelected and Theme.SoftAccentHover or Theme.Row
        row.BackgroundTransparency = (isSelected or isManual) and 0.02 or 0.14
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.Parent = panels["TargetBody"]
        if isSelected or isManual then
            corner(row, 10)
            local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            st.Color = isManual and Color3.fromRGB(56, 214, 110) or Theme.Accent
            st.Thickness = 1
            st.Transparency = 0.35
            st.Parent = row
        end

        local leftLine = Instance.new("Frame")
        leftLine.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -8)
        leftLine.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 4)
        leftLine.BackgroundColor3 = (isSelected or isManual or isPriority) and Theme.Accent or Theme.RowHover
        leftLine.BackgroundTransparency = (isSelected or isManual or isPriority) and 0 or 0.65
        leftLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
        leftLine.Parent = row
        corner(leftLine, 2)

        local rank = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        rank.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 1, 0)
        rank.Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 0)
        rank.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        rank.Text = string.format("%02d", displayIndex)
        rank.TextColor3 = (isSelected or isManual or isPriority) and Theme.AccentLight or Theme.Dim
        rank.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        rank.TextSize = 9
        rank.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        rank.Parent = row

        local nm = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nm.Size = UDim2.new(1, -145, 0, 18)
        nm.Position = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 2)
        nm.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nm.Text = pet.petName or "?"
        nm.TextColor3 = (isSelected or isManual or isPriority) and Theme.Text or Color3.fromRGB(190, 185, 205)
        nm.Font = (isSelected or isManual or isPriority) and Enum.Font.GothamBlack or Enum.Font.GothamBold
        nm.TextSize = 11
        nm.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        nm.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        nm.Parent = row

        local owner = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        owner.Size = UDim2.new(1, -150, 0, 13)
        owner.Position = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 18)
        owner.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        owner.Text = pet.owner and ("@" .. tostring(pet.owner)) or ""
        owner.TextColor3 = Theme.Dim
        owner.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        owner.TextSize = 9
        owner.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        owner.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        owner.Parent = row

        local val = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        val.Size = UDim2.new(0, 102, 1, 0)
        val.Position = UDim2.new(1, -108, 0, 0)
        val.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        val.Text = pet.mpsText or ""
        val.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(56, 214, 110)
        val.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        val.TextSize = 10
        val.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        val.Parent = row

        local overlay = Instance.new("TextButton")
        overlay.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
        overlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        overlay.Text = ""
        overlay.ZIndex = 8
        overlay.Parent = row
        overlay.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if manuallySelectedUID == pet.uid then
                manuallySelectedUID = nil
                selectedTargetUID = nil
                SharedState.SelectedPetData = nil
            else
                selectedTargetUID = pet.uid
                manuallySelectedUID = pet.uid
                SharedState.SelectedPetData = pet
                if pet then SharedState.LastTargetedPetMpsValue = pet.mpsValue or 0 end
            end
            refreshTargetPanel()

            if manuallySelectedUID then
                task.spawn(function()
                    local pr = PromptMemoryCache[pet.uid] or findProximityPromptForAnimal(pet.animalData)
                    if pr then
                        if _G.SXE_ExecuteManualSteal then
                            pcall(_G.SXE_ExecuteManualSteal, pr)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)

        makeThinSeparator(panels["TargetBody"])
    end

    local idx = 0
    for _, pet in ipairs(prioPets) do idx = idx + 1; makeTargetRow(pet, idx, true) end
    for _, pet in ipairs(otherPets) do idx = idx + 1; makeTargetRow(pet, idx, false) end

    for i = idx + 1, 10 do
        local row = Instance.new("Frame")
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 30)
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        row.Parent = panels["TargetBody"]

        local n = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        n.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 1, 0)
        n.Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 0)
        n.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        n.Text = string.format("%02d", i)
        n.TextColor3 = Theme.Dim
        n.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        n.TextSize = 9
        n.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        n.Parent = row

        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        l.Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 1, 0)
        l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 0)
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = "Scanning for Brainrot"
        l.TextColor3 = Theme.Dim
        l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        l.TextSize = 10
        l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        l.Parent = row
        makeThinSeparator(panels["TargetBody"])
    end

    makeModeFooter()
end
task.spawn(function() while true do task.wait(0.4); refreshTargetPanel() end end)
end -- END STEAL TARGET SCOPE

LazyInit("Admin Panel UI", function() -- ADMIN PANEL UI SCOPE
    pcall(function() local e=playerGui:FindFirstChild("XiAdminPanel"); if e then e:Destroy() end end)
    apGui=Instance.new("ScreenGui"); apGui.Name="XiAdminPanel"; apGui.ResetOnSpawn=false; apGui.IgnoreGuiInset=true; apGui.DisplayOrder=9999998; apGui.ZIndexBehavior=Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling; apGui.Parent=playerGui; attachCleanGui(apGui)
    apGui.Enabled = (Config.AdminPanelUI == true)
    apOuter=Instance.new("Frame"); apOuter.Name="Frame"; apOuter.BackgroundTransparency=1; apOuter.BorderSizePixel=0; apOuter.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(520,0); apOuter.AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y; apOuter.Position=UDim2.new(0.18,0,0.57,0); apOuter.ZIndex=10; apOuter.ClipsDescendants=true; apOuter.Parent=registerScreenGui(apGui)
    -- Admin panel size control. Saved scale: 0.70x to 1.50x, shown as 70% to 150%, changes in 5% steps.
    Config.AdminPanelScale = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.AdminPanelScale) or 1.00, 0.70, 1.50)
    local apScaleObj = Instance.new("UIScale")
    apScaleObj.Name = "AdminPanel_SizeScale"
    apScaleObj.Scale = Config.AdminPanelScale
    apScaleObj.Parent = apOuter
    local function setAdminPanelScale(v)
        Config.AdminPanelScale = math.clamp(v, 0.70, 1.50)
        apScaleObj.Scale = Config.AdminPanelScale
        if _G.__APScaleLabel then
            _G.__APScaleLabel.Text = tostring(math.floor((Config.AdminPanelScale * 100) + 0.5)) .. "%"
        end
        task.defer(function()
            fitPorkPanel(apOuter)
            if apOuter and apOuter.Parent then
                Config.positions = Config.positions or {}
                Config.positions.AdminPanel = serializePos(apOuter.Position)
            end
            if saveConfig then saveConfig() end
        end)
    end
    _G.setAdminPanelScale = setAdminPanelScale
    apBG=Instance.new("Frame"); apBG.BackgroundColor3=Theme.MainBackground; apBG.BackgroundTransparency=0.10; apBG.BorderSizePixel=0; apBG.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(-3,-2); apBG.Size=UDim2.new(1,6,1,4); apBG.ZIndex=0; apBG.Parent=apOuter; corner(apBG,14); addOutline(apBG)
    apTop=Instance.new("Frame"); apTop.BackgroundTransparency=1; apTop.BorderSizePixel=0; apTop.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,50); apTop.ZIndex=6; apTop.Parent=apOuter
    do -- redesigned header: pig badge + PORK HUB / Admin Command Panel + accent underline
        local badge=Instance.new("TextLabel"); badge.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(30,30); badge.Position=UDim2.new(0,12,0,10); badge.BackgroundColor3=Theme.Accent; badge.Text="🐷"; badge.TextSize=16; badge.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; badge.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1); badge.ZIndex=7; badge.Parent=apTop; corner(badge,9); stroke(badge,Theme.AccentLight,1.25,0.3)
        local title=Instance.new("TextLabel"); title.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,18); title.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,9); title.BackgroundTransparency=1; title.Text="PORK HUB"; title.TextColor3=Theme.Text; title.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; title.TextSize=14; title.ZIndex=7; title.Parent=apTop
        local sub=Instance.new("TextLabel"); sub.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,14); sub.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,28); sub.BackgroundTransparency=1; sub.Text="Admin Command Panel"; sub.TextColor3=Theme.Dim; sub.Font=Enum.Font.GothamMedium; sub.TextSize=11; sub.ZIndex=7; sub.Parent=apTop
        local under=Instance.new("Frame"); under.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0); under.Size=UDim2.new(0,66,0,3); under.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0,45); under.BackgroundColor3=Theme.Accent; under.BorderSizePixel=0; under.ZIndex=7; under.Parent=apTop; corner(under,2)

        local minus=Instance.new("TextButton"); minus.Name="AdminScaleMinus"; minus.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,0); minus.Position=UDim2.new(1,-88,0,9); minus.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(24,22); minus.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SoftButton; minus.Text="-"; minus.TextColor3=Theme.Text; minus.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; minus.TextSize=14; minus.AutoButtonColor=false; minus.ZIndex=8; minus.Parent=apTop; corner(minus,6)
        local pct=Instance.new("TextLabel"); pct.Name="AdminScalePercent"; pct.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,0); pct.Position=UDim2.new(1,-32,0,9); pct.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(54,22); pct.BackgroundTransparency=1; pct.Text=tostring(math.floor(((Config.AdminPanelScale or 1) * 100) + 0.5)).."%"; pct.TextColor3=Theme.Text; pct.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; pct.TextSize=11; pct.ZIndex=8; pct.Parent=apTop; _G.__APScaleLabel=pct
        local plus=Instance.new("TextButton"); plus.Name="AdminScalePlus"; plus.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,0); plus.Position=UDim2.new(1,-8,0,9); plus.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(24,22); plus.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SoftButton; plus.Text="+"; plus.TextColor3=Theme.Text; plus.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; plus.TextSize=14; plus.AutoButtonColor=false; plus.ZIndex=8; plus.Parent=apTop; corner(plus,6)
        minus.MouseEnter:Connect(function() minus.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SoftButtonHover end); minus.MouseLeave:Connect(function() minus.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SoftButton end)
        plus.MouseEnter:Connect(function() plus.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SoftButtonHover end); plus.MouseLeave:Connect(function() plus.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SoftButton end)
        minus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setAdminPanelScale((Config.AdminPanelScale or 1) - 0.05) end)
        plus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setAdminPanelScale((Config.AdminPanelScale or 1) + 0.05) end)
    end

    makeDraggable(apOuter,apTop,"AdminPanel"); applySavedPosition("AdminPanel",apOuter);

    apList=Instance.new("Frame"); apList.BackgroundTransparency=1; apList.BorderSizePixel=0; apList.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,54); apList.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0); apList.AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y; apList.Parent=apOuter; corner(apList,3)
    apPad=Instance.new("UIPadding"); apPad.PaddingTop=UDim.new(0,2); apPad.PaddingBottom=UDim.new(0,2); apPad.PaddingLeft=UDim.new(0,4); apPad.PaddingRight=UDim.new(0,4); apPad.Parent=apList
    Instance.new("UIListLayout",apList).SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder; apList:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout").Padding=UDim.new(0,2)

    -- COMPACT AP controls -- Click to AP + Proximity as tiny ON/OFF buttons on one
    -- line with a tiny distance slider, then a Spam Base Owner button. No glow.
    do
        local ctrl=Instance.new("Frame"); ctrl.Name="APControls"; ctrl.BackgroundTransparency=1; ctrl.Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,58); ctrl.LayoutOrder=-10; ctrl.ZIndex=6; ctrl.Parent=apList
        local function tinyToggle(px, labelText, toggleName, cb)
            regToggle(toggleName, getToggle(toggleName))

            -- Label-only toggle: no separate ON/OFF button.
            -- Clicking "Click AP" or "Prox" toggles it. The background shows enabled state.
            local btn=Instance.new("TextButton")
            btn.Name="APLabelToggle"
            btn.BackgroundTransparency=0
            btn.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(px,3)
            btn.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(104,22)
            btn.Text=labelText:gsub(":", "")
            btn.TextColor3=Theme.Text
            btn.Font=Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
            btn.TextSize=11
            btn.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            btn.AutoButtonColor=false
            btn.ZIndex=7
            btn.Parent=ctrl
            corner(btn,6)

            local function rf(v)
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = v and Theme.Green or Theme.SoftButton
                btn.TextColor3 = v and Color3.new(1,1,1) or Theme.Text
            end

            rf(getToggle(toggleName))
            onToggleChanged(toggleName, rf)

            btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local nv = not getToggle(toggleName)
                setToggle(toggleName, nv)
                if cb then cb(nv) end
            end)
        end
        tinyToggle(4, "Click AP", "Click to AP", function(on) Config.ClickToAP=on; saveConfig() end)
        tinyToggle(132, "Prox", "Proximity", function(on) setProximityAP(on) end)
        -- tiny distance slider + value on the same line
        do
            local vlbl=Instance.new("TextLabel"); vlbl.Name="DistVal"; vlbl.BackgroundTransparency=1; vlbl.Position=UDim2.new(1,-70,0,3); vlbl.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(66,20); vlbl.Text=(Config.ProximityRange or 15).." studs"; vlbl.TextColor3=Theme.Dim; vlbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamMedium; vlbl.TextSize=10; vlbl.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Right; vlbl.ZIndex=7; vlbl.Parent=ctrl
            local sbar=Instance.new("Frame"); sbar.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SliderBg; sbar.BorderSizePixel=0; sbar.Position=UDim2.new(0,262,0,12); sbar.Size=UDim2.new(1,-340,0,6); sbar.ZIndex=7; sbar.Parent=ctrl; corner(sbar,3)
            local function rel() return math.clamp(((Config.ProximityRange or 15)-1)/49,0,1) end
            local sfill=Instance.new("Frame"); sfill.BackgroundColor3=Theme.Accent; sfill.BorderSizePixel=0; sfill.Size=UDim2.new(rel(),0,1,0); sfill.ZIndex=8; sfill.Parent=sbar; corner(sfill,3)
            local sknob=Instance.new("Frame"); sknob.Name="WhiteSliderKnob"; sknob.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5); sknob.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255); sknob.BorderSizePixel=0; sknob.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(12,12); sknob.Position=UDim2.new(rel(),0,0.5,0); sknob.ZIndex=9; sknob.Parent=sbar; corner(sknob,20)
            local drag=false
            local function upd(x) local r=math.clamp((x-sbar.AbsolutePosition.X)/math.max(sbar.AbsoluteSize.X,1),0,1); local v=math.floor(1+r*49+0.5); Config.ProximityRange=v; saveConfig(); sfill.Size=UDim2.new(r,0,1,0); sknob.Position=UDim2.new(r,0,0.5,0); vlbl.Text=v.." studs" end
            sbar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then drag=true; upd(i.Position.X) end end)
            UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i) if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then drag=false end end)
            UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i) if drag and (i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then upd(i.Position.X) end end)
        end
        -- Spam Base Owner button (second line)
        local spam=Instance.new("TextButton"); spam.Name="SpamBaseOwnerBtn"; spam.Position=UDim2.new(0,4,0,30); spam.Size=UDim2.new(1,-8,0,24); spam.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SoftButton; spam.Text="Spam Base Owner"; spam.TextColor3=Theme.Text; spam.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; spam.TextSize=12; spam.AutoButtonColor=false; spam.ZIndex=7; spam.Parent=ctrl; corner(spam,6)
        spam.MouseEnter:Connect(function() spam.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SoftButtonHover end)
        spam.MouseLeave:Connect(function() spam.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SoftButton end)
        spam.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() spamBaseOwner() end)
    end

    apRows,stealLabels={},{}; rc=0
    -- Build RP_BUTTONS dynamically from Config.AdminPanelButtons
    local function buildRPButtons()
        local btns = {}
        local order = Config.AdminPanelOrder or AP_ALL_COMMANDS
        for _, cmd in ipairs(order) do
            if Config.AdminPanelButtons and Config.AdminPanelButtons[cmd] then
                table.insert(btns, {AP_COMMAND_EMOJIS[cmd] or "⚡", cmd})
            end
        end
        if #btns == 0 then btns = {{"🌀","ragdoll"},{"🔒","jail"},{"🚀","rocket"},{"🎈","balloon"}} end
        return btns
    end
    RP_BUTTONS = buildRPButtons()

    _G.refreshAdminPanelRows = function()
        for uid, row in pairs(apRows) do
            if row then row:Destroy() end
        end
        table.clear(apRows)
        table.clear(stealLabels)
        rc = 0
        RP_BUTTONS = buildRPButtons()
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= player then createAPRow(plr) end
        end
    end

    function createAPRow(plr)
        if not plr or plr==player then return end; if apRows[plr.UserId] and apRows[plr.UserId].Parent then return end
        rc=rc+1; local isAlt=(rc%2==0)
        local actW = (#RP_BUTTONS + 1) * 32 -- Buttons + Blacklist + padding
        local rowW = math.max(460, actW + 180) -- Base width 460, expand if buttons take too much space
        local row=Instance.new("Frame"); row.Name="Row_"..plr.UserId; row.BackgroundColor3=isAlt and Theme.Row or Theme.Panel; row.BackgroundTransparency=0.40; row.BorderSizePixel=0; row.Size=UDim2.new(0,rowW,0,54); row.ZIndex=5; row.ClipsDescendants=true; row.Parent=apList; corner(row,10)
        accentBar(row,true)
        apRows[plr.UserId]=row
        row.MouseEnter:Connect(function() 
            if not isPlayerBlacklisted(plr) then
                row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.RowHover
            else
                row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.BlacklistHover
            end
        end)
        row.MouseLeave:Connect(function() 
            row.BackgroundColor3 = isAlt and Theme.Row or Theme.Panel
        end)

        local avatarH=Instance.new("Frame"); avatarH.BackgroundColor3=Theme.InputBg; avatarH.BorderSizePixel=0; avatarH.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(36,36); avatarH.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(12,9); avatarH.Parent=row; corner(avatarH,10)
        local avatar=Instance.new("ImageLabel"); avatar.BackgroundTransparency=1; avatar.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1); avatar.ZIndex=10; avatar.Parent=avatarH; corner(avatar,10)
        task.spawn(function() local ok,img=pcall(function() return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(plr.UserId,Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size48x48) end); if ok then avatar.Image=img end end)
        local headshotStroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",avatarH); headshotStroke.Color=Theme.Accent; headshotStroke.Thickness=1; headshotStroke.Transparency=0.65

        local txtW=-(actW+60)
        local nameLabel=Instance.new("TextLabel"); nameLabel.Size=UDim2.new(1,txtW,0,20); nameLabel.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(50,6); nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency=1; nameLabel.Text=plr.DisplayName; nameLabel.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; nameLabel.TextSize=14; nameLabel.TextColor3=Theme.Text; nameLabel.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; nameLabel.ZIndex=10; nameLabel.Parent=row
        local userL=Instance.new("TextLabel"); userL.BackgroundTransparency=1; userL.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(50,23); userL.Size=UDim2.new(1,txtW,0,16); userL.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; userL.Text="@"..plr.Name; userL.Font=Enum.Font.GothamMedium; userL.TextSize=10; userL.TextColor3=Theme.Dim; userL.ZIndex=10; userL.Parent=row
        local statusL=Instance.new("TextLabel"); statusL.BackgroundTransparency=1; statusL.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(50,37); statusL.Size=UDim2.new(1,txtW,0,14); statusL.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; statusL.Text=""; statusL.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; statusL.TextSize=11; statusL.TextColor3=Theme.AccentLight; statusL.ZIndex=10; statusL.Parent=row
        stealLabels[plr.UserId]=statusL

        local actions=Instance.new("Frame"); actions.BackgroundTransparency=1; actions.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,0.5); actions.Position=UDim2.new(1,-8,0.5,0); actions.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(actW+10,38); actions.ZIndex=12; actions.Parent=row
        local al=Instance.new("UIListLayout"); al.FillDirection=Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal; al.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder; al.Padding=UDim.new(0,2); al.Parent=actions

        for i,b in ipairs(RP_BUTTONS) do
            local btn=Instance.new("TextButton"); btn.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(30,30); btn.BackgroundTransparency=0; btn.AutoButtonColor=false; btn.Text=b[1]; btn.TextSize=12; btn.LayoutOrder=i; btn.Parent=actions
            btn.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SoftButton; btn.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; btn.ZIndex=13
            Instance.new("UICorner",btn).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,8)

            -- Custom ragdoll image button: replaces the blue spiral text icon with the uploaded ragdoll asset.
            if b[2] == "ragdoll" then
                btn.Text = ""
                local ragdollImg = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                ragdollImg.Name = "RagdollIcon"
                ragdollImg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                ragdollImg.Image = RAGDOLL_ICON_IMAGE
                ragdollImg.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
                ragdollImg.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                ragdollImg.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
                ragdollImg.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22)
                ragdollImg.ZIndex = 14
                ragdollImg.Parent = btn
            end

            btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() btn.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SoftButtonHover end)
            btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() btn.BackgroundColor3=Theme.SoftButton end)
            btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if isPlayerBlacklisted(plr) then
                    ShowNotification("BLOCKED", plr.DisplayName .. " is blacklisted")
                    return
                end
                if apIsOnCooldown(b[2]) then return end; pcall(runAdminCommand,plr,b[2])
            end)
        end

        local blacklistBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        blacklistBtn.Name = "BlacklistBtn"
        blacklistBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30)
        blacklistBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        blacklistBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        blacklistBtn.Text = "✖"
        blacklistBtn.TextSize = 12
        blacklistBtn.LayoutOrder = 5
        blacklistBtn.Parent = actions
        blacklistBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        blacklistBtn.ZIndex = 13
        Instance.new("UICorner", blacklistBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

        local function updateBlacklistVisuals()
            local isBlacklisted = isPlayerBlacklisted(plr)
            if isBlacklisted then
                blacklistBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
                blacklistBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                
                row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.85
                avatar.ImageTransparency = 0.75
                nameLabel.TextTransparency = 0.6
                userL.TextTransparency = 0.6
                statusL.TextTransparency = 0.6
                
                for _, child in ipairs(actions:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("TextButton") and child ~= blacklistBtn then
                        child.BackgroundTransparency = 0.8
                        child.TextTransparency = 0.8
                    end
                end
            else
                blacklistBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.BlacklistLeave
                blacklistBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
                
                row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.40
                avatar.ImageTransparency = 0
                nameLabel.TextTransparency = 0
                userL.TextTransparency = 0
                statusL.TextTransparency = 0
                
                for _, child in ipairs(actions:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("TextButton") then
                        child.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                        child.TextTransparency = 0
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        blacklistBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() 
            if not isPlayerBlacklisted(plr) then
                blacklistBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.BlacklistHover
            end
        end)
        blacklistBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() 
            if not isPlayerBlacklisted(plr) then
                blacklistBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.BlacklistLeave
            end
        end)

        blacklistBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local isBlacklisted = isPlayerBlacklisted(plr)
            _G.apBlacklist[tostring(plr.UserId)] = not isBlacklisted
            Config.apBlacklist = _G.apBlacklist
            saveConfig()
            updateBlacklistVisuals()
            if not isBlacklisted then
                ShowNotification("BLACKLIST", plr.DisplayName .. " blacklisted")
            else
                ShowNotification("BLACKLIST", plr.DisplayName .. " whitelisted")
            end
        end)

        updateBlacklistVisuals()

        -- Click on player row = fire ALL available admin commands at once (like Click to AP)
        row.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
            -- Ignore clicks on the action buttons themselves
            local mousePos = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
            if actions and actions.AbsolutePosition and actions.AbsoluteSize then
                local ax, ay = actions.AbsolutePosition.X, actions.AbsolutePosition.Y
                local aw, ah = actions.AbsoluteSize.X, actions.AbsoluteSize.Y
                if mousePos.X >= ax and mousePos.X <= ax+aw and mousePos.Y >= ay and mousePos.Y <= ay+ah then
                    return
                end
            end
            if isPlayerBlacklisted(plr) then
                ShowNotification("BLOCKED", plr.DisplayName .. " is blacklisted")
                return
            end
            task.spawn(function()
                local activeCmds = {}
                for _,cmd in ipairs(AP_ALL_COMMANDS) do
                    if not apIsOnCooldown(cmd) then
                        table.insert(activeCmds, cmd)
                    end
                end
                if #activeCmds == 0 then
                    ShowNotification("ADMIN PANEL", "All commands on cooldown")
                    return
                end
                -- Fire ALL at once with minimal delay
                for i, cmd in ipairs(activeCmds) do
                    task.spawn(function()
                        task.wait((i - 1) * 0.01)
                        runAdminCommand(plr, cmd)
                    end)
                end
                ShowNotification("ADMIN PANEL", "Fired " .. #activeCmds .. " cmds on " .. plr.DisplayName)
            end)
        end)

        -- Steal / base-owner status. Reads the cached Synchronizer + memoized
        -- base-owner set, so every row shares one scan instead of each rescanning.
        task.spawn(function() while row.Parent do task.wait(0.5)
            if not plr or not plr.Parent then break end
            local st=stealLabels[plr.UserId]; if not st then break end

            local stealOwner, stealPet = getStealingInfo(plr)
            local curBaseOwnerId = _G.__getCurrentBaseOwnerId()

            if stealPet then
                local fromTxt = stealOwner and (" from " .. (stealOwner.DisplayName or stealOwner.Name)) or ""
                st.Text = "● Stealing " .. tostring(stealPet) .. fromTxt
                st.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90)
            elseif curBaseOwnerId and curBaseOwnerId == plr.UserId then
                st.Text = "● Base Owner"
                st.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 230, 120)
            else
                st.Text = ""
            end

            -- Danger-tool name highlight
            pcall(function() local ht=getHeldTool(plr)
                if ht and DANGER_TOOLS[ht] then nameLabel.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,60,60) else nameLabel.TextColor3=Theme.Text end
            end)
        end end)
    end

    for _,plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if plr~=player then createAPRow(plr) end end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr) task.defer(function() createAPRow(plr) end) end)
    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr) local row=apRows[plr.UserId]; if row then row:Destroy(); apRows[plr.UserId]=nil end; stealLabels[plr.UserId]=nil end)
end) -- END ADMIN PANEL UI SCOPE (LazyInit)

    tabBar.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, tabLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 4)

    b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftButton

    b.Text = string.upper(name)
    b.TextColor3 = Theme.Dim
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    b.TextSize = 10
    b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    b.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
