        --    gap exists that the wide berth can't use -> take the sharp gap route.
        --    Otherwise keep the wide smooth turns. This gives the most optimal of
        --    the two without ever needlessly detouring or flying over a small gap.
        local wide  = tryNav(16, 14, false)
        local tight = tryNav(7,  6,  true)
        local route
        if wide and tight then
            if _routeLen(tight) + 10 < _routeLen(wide) then
                route = tight   -- narrow-gap shortcut found -> sharp turns through it
            else
                route = wide    -- open area -> keep the big, smooth turns
            end
        else
            route = wide or tight
        end
        if route then return _avoidZones(fromPos, route) end

        -- 3) no ground path at all -> up-and-over so we never plow through a wall.
        local baseY = math.max(fromPos.Y, entry.Y)
        local cruiseY = baseY + 30
        return _avoidZones(fromPos, {
            fromPos,
            Vector3.new(fromPos.X, cruiseY, fromPos.Z),
            Vector3.new(entry.X, cruiseY, entry.Z),
            entry + Vector3.new(0, FLOAT, 0),
            toPos,
        })
    end

    -- ===== Clone / goToBrainrot =====
    -- Stored on a namespaced table instead of new top-level locals. The full hub
    -- is close to Luau's 200-local-register limit, so extra local helper names
    -- can make compilation fail even though each helper is otherwise valid.
    _G.__PorkTPHelpers = _G.__PorkTPHelpers or {}

    _G.__PorkTPHelpers.doClone = function()
        if not NetModule then pcall(loadNet) end
        local char = LP.Character or LP.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not char or not hum then return false end
        local cloner = (LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack") and LP.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner"))
                    or char:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner")
        if not cloner then return false end
        if cloner.Parent ~= char then
            pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(cloner) end)
            task.wait()
        end
        if not NetModule then return false end
        local useOk = pcall(function() NetModule:RemoteEvent("UseItem"):FireServer() end)
        task.wait(0.05)
        local telOk = pcall(function() NetModule:RemoteEvent("QuantumCloner/OnTeleport"):FireServer() end)
        return useOk and telOk
    end

    -- CARPET GLIDE: fly straight to the pet on the equipped carpet at FULL 3D speed
    -- (same pace as velMoveThrough -- WalkTPSpeed in the actual direction, vertical
    -- included). The carpet is already equipped by goToBrainrot, so no extra equip
    -- wait. Used as the FINAL approach after the clone/grabble TP.
    _G.__PorkTPHelpers.carpetGlideTo = function(targetPos)
        if not targetPos then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end)
        pcall(equipCarpet)
        local SPEED = 180  -- go-to-brainrot approach speed is fixed (not user-changeable)
        local FLOAT_OFFSET = (targetPos.Y > 20) and -4 or 0
        local goal = Vector3.new(targetPos.X, targetPos.Y + FLOAT_OFFSET, targetPos.Z)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        local lastDist, stall = math.huge, 0
        while hrp.Parent and (os.clock() - t0) < 8 do
            if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") then break end
            equipCarpet()
            local diff = goal - hrp.Position
            local mag = diff.Magnitude          -- full 3D distance (so it doesn't stop short on upper floors)
            if mag < 3 then break end
            if mag > lastDist - 0.05 then stall = stall + 1 else stall = 0 end
            lastDist = mag
            if stall >= 30 then break end       -- stuck on a wall -> give up
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = diff.Unit * SPEED   -- full speed straight at the pet
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
        if hrp and hrp.Parent then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end

    _G.__PorkTPHelpers.goToBrainrot = function(petPos)
        if not petPos then return end
        local char, hrp, hum
        local _t0 = os.clock()
        repeat
            char = LP.Character
            hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hrp and hum then break end
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        until os.clock() - _t0 > 3
        if not hrp or not hum then return end
        pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end)
        local _equipped = false
        do
            local _e0 = os.clock()
            repeat
                char = LP.Character
                for _, _cn in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
                    if char and char:FindFirstChild(_cn) then _equipped = true; break end
                end
                if _equipped then break end
                equipCarpet()
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            until _equipped or os.clock() - _e0 > 1.5
            if _equipped then task.wait(0.2) end
        end
        char = LP.Character
        hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp then return end
        pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end)
        local _plotRad = (petPos.Y <= 8.9) and 26 or 25
        do
            local _t0b = os.clock()
            repeat
                local p = hrp.Position
                local inRad = false
                local plotsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                if plotsFolder then
                    for _, plot in ipairs(plotsFolder:GetChildren()) do
                        pcall(function()
                            local pp = plot:GetPivot().Position
                            if math.abs(p.X - pp.X) < _plotRad and math.abs(p.Z - pp.Z) < _plotRad then inRad = true end
                        end)
                        if inRad then break end
                    end
                end
                if inRad then break end
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            until os.clock() - _t0b > 1.5
        end
        local h = petPos.Y
        local targetY = hrp.Position.Y
        if h > 23.15 then targetY = 21
        elseif h >= 11 and h <= 23.15 then targetY = 14.5
        elseif h >= -6.9 and h <= 8.9 then targetY = -4 end
        local _to = Vector3.new(petPos.X, targetY, petPos.Z)
        if Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.BrainrotCarpet then
            -- Carpet to Brainrot: after the clone/grabble TP into the base, glide on
            -- the carpet straight to the pet (uses live Walk To Brainrot Speed).
            _G.__PorkTPHelpers.carpetGlideTo(petPos)
        else
            local _route = computeRoute(hrp.Position, _to, nil)
            if not _route or #_route == 0 then _route = { _to } end
            velMoveThrough(hrp, _route, 180, true, true)  -- fixed go-to-brainrot speed (not user-changeable)
        end
        if hrp and hrp.Parent then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        do
            local _platPos = (hrp and hrp.Parent and hrp.Position) or _to
            local _feetY = _platPos.Y - 3
            local _plat = Instance.new("Part")
            _plat.Name = "SXETempPlatform"; _plat.Size = Vector3.new(8, 1, 8)
            _plat.Position = Vector3.new(petPos.X, _feetY - 1.5, petPos.Z)
            _plat.Anchored = true; _plat.CanCollide = false; pcall(makeOneWay, _plat); _plat.Transparency = 1
            _plat.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic; _plat.Parent = workspace
            task.spawn(function()
                local _s = tick()
                while tick() - _s < 20 do
                    if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") then break end
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
                if _plat and _plat.Parent then _plat:Destroy() end
            end)
        end
    end

    -- ===== Steal engine =====
    InternalStealCache = {}
    STEAL_HOLD_DURATION = 1.3
    _stealHoldStart = 0
    _stealHoldActive = false

    function buildStealCallbacks(prompt)
        if InternalStealCache[prompt] then return end
        if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then return end
        local data = { holdCallbacks = {}, triggerCallbacks = {}, holdEndCallbacks = {}, ready = true }
        local function grab(sig, into)
            local ok, conns = pcall(getconnections, sig)
            if ok and type(conns) == "table" then
                for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
                    if type(c.Function) == "function" then table.insert(into, c.Function) end
                end
            end
        end
        grab(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan, data.holdCallbacks)
        grab(prompt.Triggered, data.triggerCallbacks)
        grab(prompt.PromptButtonHoldEnded, data.holdEndCallbacks)
        if #data.holdCallbacks > 0 or #data.triggerCallbacks > 0 or #data.holdEndCallbacks > 0 then
            InternalStealCache[prompt] = data
        end
    end

    function executeStealAsync(prompt)
        local data = InternalStealCache[prompt]
        if not data or not data.ready then return false end
        data.ready = false
        _stealHoldStart = tick()
        _stealHoldActive = true
        _G.SXE_StealStatus = _G.SXE_StealStatus or {}
        _G.SXE_StealStatus.active = true
        _G.SXE_StealStatus.start = _stealHoldStart
        _G.SXE_StealStatus.duration = STEAL_HOLD_DURATION

        task.spawn(function()
            for _, fn in ipairs(data.holdCallbacks) do task.spawn(fn) end
            pcall(function()
                local _st = prompt:GetAttribute("State")
                if _st ~= nil and _st ~= "Steal" then
                    if not _G._xenStealRemote then
                        local _net = _G.XenNet or require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Packages"):WaitForChild("Net"):FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ModuleScript", true))
                        _G._xenStealRemote = _net and _net:RemoteEvent("f40f7d9e-2f0d-4167-b250-899273f46874")
                    end
                    local r = _G._xenStealRemote
                    if r then
                        local _t = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() + 124
                        r:FireServer(_t, "68c86eb7-eb7e-4b4d-96ae-cf7cd847c5b0")
                        r:FireServer(_t, "07b9cc25-2a1f-4a26-a0ec-f2fab578d8bd")
                    end
                end
            end)
            local remain = STEAL_HOLD_DURATION - (tick() - _stealHoldStart)
            if remain > 0 then task.wait(remain) end
            if prompt and prompt.Parent then
                for _, fn in ipairs(data.triggerCallbacks) do task.spawn(fn) end
            end
            for _, fn in ipairs(data.holdEndCallbacks) do task.spawn(fn) end
            _stealHoldActive = false
            if _G.SXE_StealStatus then _G.SXE_StealStatus.active = false end
            task.wait(0.05)
            data.ready = true
        end)
        return true
    end

    function findStealPrompt(pet)
        if pet.plot and pet.slot then
            local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(pet.plot)
            local podiums = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            local podium = podiums and podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(pet.slot))
            if podium then
                local base = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
                local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                local attach = spawn and spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                if attach then
                    for _, p in ipairs(attach:GetChildren()) do
                        if p:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return p end
                    end
                end
                for _, d in ipairs(podium:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return d end
                end
            end
        end
        if pet.model and pet.model.Parent then
            for _, d in ipairs(pet.model:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return d end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    STEAL_PROXIMITY = 60
    STEAL_ARM_TIMEOUT = 25
    _stealTarget = nil
    _stealArmedAt = 0

    function timeUntilCanSteal()
        if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") or LP:GetAttribute("IsTrading")
            or LP:GetAttribute("IsDuelSelecting") or LP:GetAttribute("Web") then
            return -1
        end
        return 0
    end

    function _petKey(pet)
        if not pet then return nil end
        if pet.plot and pet.slot then return tostring(pet.plot) .. "|" .. tostring(pet.slot) end
        return "idx|" .. tostring(pet.index or pet.name)
    end
    function _petStillExists(sel, pets)
        if not sel or not pets then return nil end
        local k = _petKey(sel)
        for _, p in ipairs(pets) do if _petKey(p) == k then return p end end
        return nil
    end
    function _pickByMode(pets)
        if not pets or #pets == 0 then return nil end
        local C = Config or {}
        -- PRIORITY FIRST: honored when EITHER the Auto-TP "Priority Mode" OR the
        -- Steal "Priority" mode is on (the two tabs are now synced here). Follow
        -- the user's priorityList order (1 -> 2 -> 3 ...), match by DisplayName OR
        -- Index, and ALWAYS take the TOPMOST priority entry present on the server,
        -- regardless of distance/gen. So with Headless Horseman at #1, any Headless
        -- on the server is taken and everything else is ignored.
        local priorityMode = C.AutoTPPriority or (C.StealMode == "Priority")
        if priorityMode and priorityList and #priorityList > 0 then
            for _, pName in ipairs(priorityList) do
                local searchName = pName:lower()
                for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
                    if (p.name and p.name:lower() == searchName) or (p.index and p.index:lower() == searchName) then
                        return p
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        -- HIGHEST gen/value: Auto-TP Highest Gen/Value OR Steal "Highest".
        if C.AutoTPHighestGen or C.AutoTPHighestValue or (C.StealMode == "Highest") then
            local best, bv
            for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
                local v = p.mps or 0
                if not bv or v > bv then bv, best = v, p end
            end
            return best
        end
        return pets[1]
    end
    function _samePet(a, b)
        if not a or not b then return false end
        return a.plot == b.plot and tostring(a.slot) == tostring(b.slot)
    end

    _stealTarget2 = nil
    function armSteal(pet)
        if not pet then return end
        _stealTarget = pet; _stealArmedAt = os.clock()
        _stealTarget2 = pet
        _G.SXE_StealStatus = _G.SXE_StealStatus or {}
        _G.SXE_StealStatus.target = pet
    end
    function disarmSteal()
        _stealTarget = nil
        _G.SXE_StealStatus = _G.SXE_StealStatus or {}
        _G.SXE_StealStatus.target = nil
        _G.SXE_StealStatus.active = false
    end
    _G.SXEArmSteal = armSteal
    _G.SXEDisarmSteal = disarmSteal

    AUTO_STEAL = (Config and Config.AutoStealEnabled) and true or false
    _G.SXEAutoSteal = function(on) AUTO_STEAL = on ~= false end

    _stealLastScan = 0
    _autoLastScan = 0
    isTeleporting = false
    _tpStartedAt = 0
    _cloneTP = false
    _cloneFired = false
    _started = false

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local now = os.clock()
        if AUTO_STEAL and _started and not LP:GetAttribute("Stealing")
            and not (isTeleporting and _cloneTP)
            and (now - _autoLastScan) >= 0.1 then
            _autoLastScan = now
            local char = LP.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                local ok, pets = pcall(scanAllPets)
                if ok and pets then
                    local best
                    -- Manual selection from SXEHub
                    if manuallySelectedUID then
                        for _, pet in ipairs(pets) do
                            if not pet.conveyor and pet.plot and pet.slot then
                                local uid = pet.plot .. "_" .. tostring(pet.slot)
                                if uid == manuallySelectedUID then best = pet; break end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if not best then
                        local STEAL_MODE = (Config and Config.StealMode) or "Priority"
                        if STEAL_MODE == "Nearest" then
                            local bd
                            for _, pet in ipairs(pets) do
                                if not pet.conveyor then
                                    local prompt = findStealPrompt(pet)
                                    if prompt and prompt.Parent then
                                        local pp = prompt.Parent
                                        local ppPos = (pp:IsA("BasePart") and pp.Position)
                                            or (pp.Parent and pp.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and pp.Parent.Position)
                                        if ppPos then
                                            local d = (hrp.Position - ppPos).Magnitude
                                            if (not bd or d < bd) then bd, best = d, pet end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            local nc = {}
                            for _, p in ipairs(pets) do if not p.conveyor then nc[#nc + 1] = p end end
                            best = _pickByMode(nc)
                        end
                    end
                    if best then
                        if not _samePet(best, _stealTarget) then armSteal(best) end
                    elseif _stealTarget and not _stealHoldActive then
                        disarmSteal()
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local pet = _stealTarget
        if not pet then return end
        local STEAL_MODE = (Config and Config.StealMode) or "Priority"
        if STEAL_MODE == "Nearest" and now - _stealArmedAt > STEAL_ARM_TIMEOUT then
            disarmSteal(); return
        end
        if now - _stealLastScan < 0.067 then return end
        _stealLastScan = now
        local t = timeUntilCanSteal()
        if t == -1 then
            if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") then disarmSteal() end
            return
        end
        if t > 0 and t > STEAL_HOLD_DURATION then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        local prompt = findStealPrompt(pet)
        if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then return end
        local pp = prompt.Parent
        local ppPos = (pp and pp:IsA("BasePart") and pp.Position)
            or (pp and pp.Parent and pp.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and pp.Parent.Position)
        local _inCloneTP = isTeleporting and _cloneTP and _cloneFired
        if not _inCloneTP and ppPos and (hrp.Position - ppPos).Magnitude > STEAL_PROXIMITY then return end
        local oldMax
        pcall(function() oldMax = prompt.MaxActivationDistance end)
        pcall(function() prompt.MaxActivationDistance = math.huge end)
        buildStealCallbacks(prompt)
        if InternalStealCache[prompt] then executeStealAsync(prompt) end
        pcall(function() if oldMax ~= nil then prompt.MaxActivationDistance = oldMax end end)
    end)

    -- ===== Main TP function (1:1 xentp.lua doVelocityTP) =====
    function doVelocityTP()
        -- Do not let selecting Flying Carpet/Cupid/Sleigh/Broom/Waverider disable Grapple TP.
        if Config and Config.TpSettings then
            Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTP = true
            Config.TpSettings.BrainrotCarpet = true
            Config.TpSettings.FlyTP = false
        end
        if isTeleporting and (os.clock() - _tpStartedAt) < 30 then return end
        isTeleporting = true
        _tpStartedAt = os.clock()
        _cloneTP = false
        _cloneFired = false
        clearViz()
        if not NetModule then pcall(loadNet) end

        local char = LP.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not hum then isTeleporting = false; return end

        local prebuilt = _G.__PORK_FAST_LOAD_PETS
        local allPets

        if prebuilt
            and type(prebuilt.pets) == "table"
            and (os.clock() - (prebuilt.capturedAt or 0)) <= 3 then
            allPets = prebuilt.pets
            _G.__PORK_FAST_LOAD_PETS = nil
        else
            allPets = scanAllPets()
        end

        if #allPets == 0 then
            local _t0 = os.clock()
            while #allPets == 0 and os.clock() - _t0 < 2.5 do
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                allPets = scanAllPets()
            end
        end
        if #allPets == 0 then isTeleporting = false; return end

        -- Respect SXEHub manual selection, then fall back to priority/mode
        local pet
        if manuallySelectedUID then
            for _, p in ipairs(allPets) do
                if p.plot and p.slot and (p.plot .. "_" .. tostring(p.slot)) == manuallySelectedUID then pet = p; break end
            end
        end
        if not pet then pet = _pickByMode(allPets) or allPets[1] end

        local _tpSpd = math.clamp(tonumber((Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed) or 600) or 600, 50, 600)
        local _cloneDelay = (Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.CloneDelayVal) or 0.35

        local petPos = pet.position
        local petName = pet.name

        local adjY = petPos.Y
        if RouteCfg.TALL_PETS[petName] then adjY = petPos.Y - RouteCfg.TALL_OFFSET end
        local coordTable = adjY > RouteCfg.UPPER_Y_THRESHOLD and UPPER or LOWER

        -- CONVEYOR
        if pet.conveyor then
            local model = pet.model
            local maxHP = hum.MaxHealth
            hum.Health = maxHP
            local healConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if hum and hum.Parent then hum.Health = maxHP end
            end)
            carpetEngage()
            vZero(hrp)
            local function livePos()
                if not model or not model.Parent then return nil end
                local part = model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                return part and part.Position or nil
            end
            local _t0 = os.clock()
            while os.clock() - _t0 < 8 do 
                if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then break end
                local lp = livePos()
                if not lp then break end
                local diff = lp - hrp.Position
                if diff.Magnitude <= 6 then break end
                equipCarpet()
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = diff.Unit * _tpSpd
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end
            if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            healConn:Disconnect()
            isTeleporting = false
            return
        end

        -- FIRST FLOOR
        if petPos.Y <= 8.9 and isPlotUnlocked(pet.plot) then
            local maxHP = hum.MaxHealth
            hum.Health = maxHP
            local healConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if hum and hum.Parent then hum.Health = maxHP end
            end)
            carpetEngage()
            vZero(hrp)
            local _to = Vector3.new(petPos.X, -4, petPos.Z)
            local _faceDir
            do
                local idx = getClosestBaseIdx(petPos)
                local _, frontFace = buildFrontCandidate(idx, false, hrp.Position.Z)
                _faceDir = frontFace
            end
            local route = computeRoute(hrp.Position, _to, _faceDir)
            if not route or #route == 0 then route = { _to } end
            velMoveThrough(hrp, route, _tpSpd, true, true)
            if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            healConn:Disconnect()
            isTeleporting = false
            return
        end

        -- SKY CLONE-TP
        _cloneTP = true
        disarmSteal()

        local closestData, skyKey = findClosest(petPos, coordTable)
        if not closestData or not skyKey then isTeleporting = false; return end

        local destPos = closestData.coord
        local maxHP = hum.MaxHealth
        hum.Health = maxHP
        local healConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if hum and hum.Parent then hum.Health = maxHP end
        end)

        carpetEngage()
        vZero(hrp)

        local facingDir = closestData.facing == "NORTH" and Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
        do
            local isUpper = (coordTable == UPPER)
            local idx = getClosestBaseIdx(petPos)
            local frontCoord, frontFace = buildFrontCandidate(idx, isUpper, hrp.Position.Z)
            local bestCoord, bestFace = frontCoord, frontFace
            local bestDist = (hrp.Position - frontCoord).Magnitude
            for _, d in ipairs(plotSides(coordTable, idx)) do
                local dd = (hrp.Position - d.coord).Magnitude
                if dd < bestDist then
                    bestDist = dd
                    bestCoord = d.coord
                    bestFace = d.facing == "NORTH" and Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
                end
            end
            destPos = bestCoord
            facingDir = bestFace
        end

        if hrp and hrp.Parent then
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + facingDir)
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end

        local _route = computeRoute(hrp.Position, destPos, facingDir)
        local _stepped = {}
        do
            local startY = hrp.Position.Y
            local destY = destPos.Y
            local prev = hrp.Position
            local totalFlat = 0
            for _, wp in ipairs(_route) do
                totalFlat = totalFlat + (Vector3.new(wp.X, 0, wp.Z) - Vector3.new(prev.X, 0, prev.Z)).Magnitude
                prev = wp
            end
            if totalFlat < 0.01 then totalFlat = 0.01 end
            local SEG = 30
            prev = hrp.Position
            local travelled = 0
            for _, wp in ipairs(_route) do
                local flatVec = Vector3.new(wp.X, 0, wp.Z) - Vector3.new(prev.X, 0, prev.Z)
                local legFlat = flatVec.Magnitude
                if legFlat >= 0.01 then
                    local subs = math.max(1, math.ceil(legFlat / SEG))
                    for s = 1, subs do
                        local f = s / subs
                        local px = prev.X + (wp.X - prev.X) * f
                        local pz = prev.Z + (wp.Z - prev.Z) * f
                        local along = travelled + legFlat * f
                        local rampY = startY + (destY - startY) * (along / totalFlat)
                        _stepped[#_stepped + 1] = Vector3.new(px, rampY, pz)
                    end
                else
                    _stepped[#_stepped + 1] = wp
                end
                travelled = travelled + legFlat
                prev = wp
            end
            if #_stepped > 0 then _stepped[#_stepped] = _route[#_route] end
        end
        velMoveThrough(hrp, _stepped, _tpSpd, true, true)

        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + facingDir)
        vZero(hrp)

        local syncFrames = 5
        local syncConn
        syncConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then syncConn:Disconnect(); return end
            syncFrames = syncFrames - 1
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(destPos, destPos + facingDir)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            if syncFrames <= 0 then syncConn:Disconnect() end
        end)

        for _ = 1, 20 do
            task.wait(0.05)
            if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then break end
        end

        healConn:Disconnect()
        armSteal(pet)
        _cloneFired = true

        local _ahrp = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local _clonePos = (_ahrp and _ahrp.Parent and _ahrp.Position) or destPos

        local _clonePlat = Instance.new("Part")
        _clonePlat.Name = "SXEClonePlatform"
        _clonePlat.Size = Vector3.new(12, 1, 12)
        _clonePlat.Position = Vector3.new(_clonePos.X, _clonePos.Y - 3, _clonePos.Z)
        _clonePlat.Anchored = true; _clonePlat.CanCollide = false; pcall(makeOneWay, _clonePlat); _clonePlat.Transparency = 1
        _clonePlat.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic; _clonePlat.Parent = workspace

        if _ahrp and _ahrp.Parent then
            _ahrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            _ahrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            pcall(function() _ahrp.Anchored = true end)
            task.delay(1, function()
                if _ahrp and _ahrp.Parent then pcall(function() _ahrp.Anchored = false end) end
            end)
        end

        local _charAdded = false
        local _caConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() _charAdded = true end)

        task.wait(_cloneDelay)
        local _cloneOk = _G.__PorkTPHelpers.doClone()

        if _clonePlat then pcall(function() _clonePlat:Destroy() end); _clonePlat = nil end
        if _cloneOk then
            task.wait(0.3)
            local _cloneSucceeded = false
            do
                local _c = LP.Character
                local _h = _c and _c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local plotsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                if _h and plotsFolder then
                    local _rad = (petPos.Y <= 8.9) and 26 or 25
                    local p = _h.Position
                    for _, plot in ipairs(plotsFolder:GetChildren()) do
                        pcall(function()
                            local pp = plot:GetPivot().Position
                            if math.abs(p.X - pp.X) < _rad and math.abs(p.Z - pp.Z) < _rad then
                                _cloneSucceeded = true
                            end
                        end)
                        if _cloneSucceeded then break end
                    end
                end
            end
            if _caConn then _caConn:Disconnect() end
            if _cloneSucceeded then _G.__PorkTPHelpers.goToBrainrot(petPos) end
        else
            if _caConn then _caConn:Disconnect() end
        end
        isTeleporting = false
    end

    doGrabbleVelocityTP = doVelocityTP
    _G.SXEStartSideTP = doVelocityTP

    -- Exact readiness point: fire the queued load TP now, rather than waiting
    -- for the remainder of the script or another polling interval.
    if _G.__PORK_TRY_FAST_LOAD_TP then
        _G.__PORK_TRY_FAST_LOAD_TP()
    end

    _G.SXE_ExecuteManualTP = function()
        task.spawn(function() pcall(doVelocityTP) end)
    end

    UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
        if gameProcessed then return end
        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.T then
            task.spawn(function() pcall(doVelocityTP) end)
        end
    end)

    -- Boot
    task.spawn(function() pcall(loadModules) pcall(loadNet) end)
    task.spawn(function()
        local char = LP.Character or LP.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
        char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
        pcall(loadModules); pcall(loadNet)
        local _t0 = os.clock()
        repeat
            local ok, pets = pcall(scanAllPets)
            if ok and pets and #pets > 0 then break end
            task.wait(0.3)
        until os.clock() - _t0 > 12
        _started = true
    end)
end

        CreditsLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        CreditsLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.15
        CreditsLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
        CreditsLabel.TextSize = 16
        CreditsLabel.TextTransparency = 0





        
    end
end)

        -- ===================== EMOJI HELPERS =====================
        local function getMutationEmoji(mutation)
            if not mutation or mutation == "" or mutation == "None" or mutation == "Default" then return "" end
            return MUTATION_EMOJIS[mutation] or ""
        end

        local function getTraitEmojiStr(traitsTable)
            if not traitsTable or #traitsTable == 0 then return "" end
            local emojis = {}
            for _, name in ipairs(traitsTable) do
                local e = TRAIT_EMOJIS[name]
                if e then table.insert(emojis, e) end
            end
            return #emojis > 0 and table.concat(emojis, " ") or ""
        end

        -- ===================== THUMBNAIL =====================
        local thumbnailCache = {}

        local function getThumbnailUrl(name)
            if not name or name == "" then return nil end
            if thumbnailCache[name] ~= nil then return thumbnailCache[name] or nil end
            local encoded = name:gsub(" ", "%%20")
            local url = "https://stealabrainrot.fandom.com/api.php?action=query&prop=pageimages&format=json&piprop=thumbnail&pithumbsize=500&titles=" .. encoded
            local ok, result = pcall(function()
                local res = stealRequest({ Url = url, Method = "GET" })
                if res and res.StatusCode == 200 and res.Body then
                    local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(res.Body)
                    local pages = data and data.query and data.query.pages
                    if pages then
                        for _, page in pairs(pages) do
                            if page.thumbnail and page.thumbnail.source then
                                return page.thumbnail.source
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            local final = (ok and result) or nil
            thumbnailCache[name] = final or false
            return final
        end

        -- ===================== GC GEN/S DATA =====================
        local BrainrotData = nil

        local function formatValue(n)
            local abs = math.abs(n)
            if abs >= 1e12 then return string.format("%.2fT", n / 1e12) end
            if abs >= 1e9  then return string.format("%.2fB", n / 1e9)  end
            if abs >= 1e6  then return string.format("%.2fM", n / 1e6)  end
            if abs >= 1e3  then return string.format("%.2fK", n / 1e3)  end
            return tostring(math.floor(n))
        end

        local function findBrainrotData()
            if type(getgc) ~= "function" then return nil end

            local okGc, objects = pcall(getgc, true)
            if not okGc or type(objects) ~= "table" then return nil end

            for _, obj in ipairs(objects) do
                if type(obj) == "table" then
                    local okCandidate, isCandidate = pcall(function()
                        -- Some getgc tables use custom __index metamethods. Accessing
                        -- missing keys normally can invoke game Signal objects and error,
                        -- so inspect only raw table contents.
                        local firstKey = next(obj)
                        if type(firstKey) ~= "string" then return false end

                        local entry = rawget(obj, firstKey)
                        if type(entry) ~= "table" then return false end

                        local generation = rawget(entry, "Generation")
                        local displayName = rawget(entry, "DisplayName")

                        return type(generation) == "number"
                            and (type(displayName) == "string" or displayName ~= nil)
                    end)

                    if okCandidate and isCandidate then
                        return obj
                    end
                end
            end

            return nil
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            for i = 1, 12 do
                BrainrotData = findBrainrotData()
                if BrainrotData then
                    print("[PORK] ✅ GC base data linked (Gen/s enabled)")
                    break
                end
                task.wait(1)
            end
            if not BrainrotData then
                warn("[PORK] ⚠️ Could not find Gen/s data")
            end
        end)

        -- Load the same generation calculator Pork Hub already uses.
        local TrackerAnimalsData, TrackerAnimalsShared
        pcall(function()
            local Datas = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Datas")
            local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared")
            if Datas and Datas:FindFirstChild("Animals") then
                TrackerAnimalsData = require(Datas.Animals)
            end
            if Shared and Shared:FindFirstChild("Animals") then
                TrackerAnimalsShared = require(Shared.Animals)
            end
        end)

        local KNOWN_MUTATIONS = {
            "Radioactive", "Yin Yang", "YinYang", "Yin-Yang", "Bloodrot",
            "Rainbow", "Galactic", "Galaxy", "Diamond", "Divine", "Cursed",
            "Cyber", "Candy", "Lava", "Gold", "Normal", "Default"
        }

        local function parseDisplayedStealName(rawName)
            local clean = tostring(rawName or "")
                :gsub("<[^>]->", "")
                :gsub("\\n", " ")
                :gsub("%s+", " ")
                :match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""

            -- Preserve an exact generation value shown by the game's steal message.
            local shownNumber, shownSuffix = clean:match("([%d%.]+)%s*([KkMmBbTt])%s*/?[sS]?%s*$")
            local shownGen
            if shownNumber then
                local mult = ({k=1e3, m=1e6, b=1e9, t=1e12})[shownSuffix:lower()]
                shownGen = tonumber(shownNumber) and tonumber(shownNumber) * mult or nil
                clean = clean:gsub("%s*[%$]?[%d%.]+%s*[KkMmBbTt]%s*/?[sS]?%s*$", "")
            end

            clean = clean:gsub("^%s*[%-%:]+%s*", ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or clean

            local mutation = "None"
            local lower = clean:lower()
            for _, candidate in ipairs(KNOWN_MUTATIONS) do
                local cLower = candidate:lower()
                if lower:sub(1, #cLower) == cLower
                    and (lower:sub(#cLower + 1, #cLower + 1) == " " or #lower == #cLower) then
                    mutation = candidate
                    clean = clean:sub(#candidate + 1):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or clean
                    break
                end
            end

            return clean ~= "" and clean or tostring(rawName or "Unknown Brainrot"), mutation, shownGen
        end

        local function findAnimalIndex(displayName)
            if not displayName then return nil end
            local wanted = tostring(displayName):lower():gsub("[%s%p]", "")

            if TrackerAnimalsData then
                for index, info in pairs(TrackerAnimalsData) do
                    local shown = type(info) == "table" and (info.DisplayName or index) or index
                    local normalized = tostring(shown):lower():gsub("[%s%p]", "")
                    if normalized == wanted then return index end
                end
            end

            if BrainrotData then
                for index, info in next, BrainrotData do
                    if type(index) == "string" then
                        local shown = type(info) == "table" and rawget(info, "DisplayName") or index
                        local normalized = tostring(shown or index):lower():gsub("[%s%p]", "")
                        if normalized == wanted then return index end
                    end
                end
            end
            return displayName
        end

        local function getBaseGeneration(displayName)
            if not BrainrotData or not displayName then return nil end
            local info = rawget(BrainrotData, displayName)
            if not info then
                local wanted = tostring(displayName):lower():gsub("[%s%p]", "")
                pcall(function()
                    for k, v in next, BrainrotData do
                        local shown = type(v) == "table" and rawget(v, "DisplayName") or k
                        if tostring(shown or k):lower():gsub("[%s%p]", "") == wanted then
                            info = v
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            return type(info) == "table" and rawget(info, "Generation") or nil
        end

        -- ===================== MUTATION & TRAIT DETECTION =====================
        local function getMutationFromModel(model)
            if not model then return "None" end

            local attr = model:GetAttribute("Mutation") or model:GetAttribute("mutation")
            if attr and tostring(attr) ~= "" then return tostring(attr) end

            for _, c in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                if c.Name:sub(1, 10) == "Mutation." then
                    return c.Name:sub(11)
                end
                if c.Name == "Mutation" then
                    if c:IsA("StringValue") and c.Value ~= "" then return c.Value end
                    local nested = c:GetAttribute("Name") or c:GetAttribute("Mutation")
                    if nested and tostring(nested) ~= "" then return tostring(nested) end
                end
            end
            return "None"
        end

        local function getTraitsFromModel(model)
            if not model then return {} end
            local traits, seen = {}, {}

            local attr = model:GetAttribute("Traits")
            if type(attr) == "string" then
                for name in attr:gmatch("[^,]+") do
                    name = name:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
                    if name ~= "" and not seen[name] then
                        seen[name] = true
                        table.insert(traits, name)
                    end
                end
            end

            for _, c in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                if c.Name:sub(1, 7) == "Trait." then
                    local t = c.Name:sub(8)
                    if not seen[t] then
                        seen[t] = true
                        table.insert(traits, t)
                    end
                end
            end
            return traits
        end

        local function normalizeTrackerName(value)
            return tostring(value or ""):lower():gsub("[%s%p]", "")
        end

        -- Track every synchronized animal separately. This lets the detector
        -- use the exact plot/slot entry that disappears when the steal completes.
        local recentAnimalHistory = {}
        local previousLiveAnimals = {}

        local function copyAnimalEntry(entry)
            if type(entry) ~= "table" then return nil end
            local copy = {}
            for key, value in pairs(entry) do
                if type(value) == "table" then
                    local nested = {}
                    for k, v in pairs(value) do nested[k] = v end
                    copy[key] = nested
                else
                    copy[key] = value
                end
            end
            copy.__capturedAt = os.clock()
            return copy
        end

        local function animalIdentity(entry)
            if type(entry) ~= "table" then return nil end
            if entry.uid and tostring(entry.uid) ~= "" then
                return tostring(entry.uid)
            end
            return table.concat({
                tostring(entry.plot or "?"),
                tostring(entry.slot or "?"),
                tostring(entry.index or entry.name or "?")
            }, "|")
        end

        local function snapshotAnimalCache()
            if type(SharedState) ~= "table"
                or type(SharedState.AllAnimalsCache) ~= "table" then
                return
            end

            local now = os.clock()
            local currentLive = {}

            for _, entry in ipairs(SharedState.AllAnimalsCache) do
                if type(entry) == "table" and entry.name then
                    local id = animalIdentity(entry)
                    if id then
                        currentLive[id] = true
                        local copy = copyAnimalEntry(entry)
                        copy.__removedAt = nil
                        recentAnimalHistory[id] = copy
                    end
                end
            end

            for id in pairs(previousLiveAnimals) do
                if not currentLive[id] then
                    local old = recentAnimalHistory[id]
                    if old and not old.__removedAt then
                        old.__removedAt = now
                    end
                end
            end

            previousLiveAnimals = currentLive

            for id, entry in pairs(recentAnimalHistory) do
                local ageBase = entry.__removedAt or entry.__capturedAt
                if not ageBase or (now - ageBase) > 20 then
                    recentAnimalHistory[id] = nil
                end
            end
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            while true do
                pcall(snapshotAnimalCache)
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end)

        local function findExactCachedAnimal(displayName, shownGeneration)
            local wanted = normalizeTrackerName(displayName)
            local now = os.clock()
            local best = nil
            local bestScore = -math.huge

            local function scoreEntry(entry)
                if type(entry) ~= "table"
                    or normalizeTrackerName(entry.name) ~= wanted then
                    return
                end

                local score = 0
                local removedAge = entry.__removedAt and (now - entry.__removedAt) or nil

                if removedAge and removedAge >= 0 and removedAge <= 4 then
                    score = score + 100000 - (removedAge * 1000)
                end

                if entry.owner
                    and entry.owner ~= LocalPlayer.Name
                    and entry.owner ~= LocalPlayer.DisplayName then
                    score = score + 10000
                end

                if type(shownGeneration) == "number"
                    and type(entry.genValue) == "number" then
                    local difference = math.abs(entry.genValue - shownGeneration)
                    local relative = difference / math.max(1, shownGeneration)
                    score = score + math.max(0, 5000 - relative * 5000)
                end

                score = score + (entry.__capturedAt or 0) * 0.0001

                if score > bestScore then
                    bestScore = score
                    best = entry
                end
            end

            for _, entry in pairs(recentAnimalHistory) do
                scoreEntry(entry)
            end

            if type(SharedState) == "table"
                and type(SharedState.AllAnimalsCache) == "table" then
                for _, entry in ipairs(SharedState.AllAnimalsCache) do
                    scoreEntry(entry)
                end
            end

            return best
        end

        local function traitsFromCache(value)
            if type(value) == "table" then
                local result = {}
                for _, trait in ipairs(value) do
                    if tostring(trait) ~= "" then
                        table.insert(result, tostring(trait))
                    end
                end
                return result
            end

            local result = {}
            if type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" and value ~= "None" then
                for trait in value:gmatch("[^,]+") do
                    trait = trait:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
                    if trait ~= "" then table.insert(result, trait) end
                end
            end
            return result
        end

        local function getDataFromModel(rawDisplayName, model)
            local displayName, parsedMutation, shownGeneration = parseDisplayedStealName(rawDisplayName)
            local cached = findExactCachedAnimal(displayName, shownGeneration)

            local modelMutation = getMutationFromModel(model)
            local mutation = modelMutation ~= "None" and modelMutation or parsedMutation
            local traits = getTraitsFromModel(model)
            local generation = shownGeneration
            local animalIndex = nil

            -- The synchronized plot cache contains the real AnimalList record,
            -- including mutation, traits and already-calculated generation.
            if cached then
                animalIndex = cached.index

                if cached.mutation
                    and tostring(cached.mutation) ~= ""
                    and tostring(cached.mutation) ~= "None" then
                    mutation = tostring(cached.mutation)
                end

                local cachedTraits = traitsFromCache(cached.traits)
                if #cachedTraits > 0 then
                    traits = cachedTraits
                end

                if type(cached.genValue) == "number" and cached.genValue > 0 then
                    generation = cached.genValue
                end
            end

            if not generation and TrackerAnimalsShared
                and type(TrackerAnimalsShared.GetGeneration) == "function" then
                local index = animalIndex
                    or (model and model.Name)
                    or findAnimalIndex(displayName)

                pcall(function()
                    generation = TrackerAnimalsShared:GetGeneration(
                        index,
                        mutation ~= "None" and mutation or nil,
                        #traits > 0 and traits or nil,
                        nil
                    )
                end)
            end

            if not generation then
                generation = getBaseGeneration(displayName)
            end

            local genText = type(generation) == "number"
                and ("$" .. formatValue(generation) .. "/s")
                or "Unknown"

            print(string.format(
                "[PORK Debug] %s | Gen/s: %s | Mutation: %s | Traits: %s | Cache: %s",
                displayName,
                genText,
                mutation,
                #traits > 0 and table.concat(traits, ", ") or "None",
                cached and "matched" or "missed"
            ))

            return genText, mutation, traits, displayName
        end

        -- ===================== WEBHOOK SENDER =====================
        local __lastWebhookKey, __lastWebhookAt = nil, 0
        local function sendStealWebhook(brainrotName, plotName, model)
            local dedupeKey = tostring(brainrotName or "Unknown")
            local now = os.clock()
            if __lastWebhookKey == dedupeKey and (now - __lastWebhookAt) < 4 then
                return
            end
            __lastWebhookKey, __lastWebhookAt = dedupeKey, now

            local genText, mutation, traits, normalizedName = getDataFromModel(brainrotName, model)
            local mutEmoji    = getMutationEmoji(mutation)
            local traitEmojis = getTraitEmojiStr(traits)
            local finalName   = normalizedName or brainrotName
            local imageUrl    = getThumbnailUrl(finalName)

            local petTitle = (mutEmoji ~= "" and mutEmoji .. " " or "") .. finalName
            if traitEmojis ~= "" then petTitle = petTitle .. " " .. traitEmojis end

            local mutDisplay = "None"
            if mutation ~= "None" and mutation ~= "" then
                mutDisplay = mutEmoji ~= "" and mutEmoji or mutation
            end

            local traitDisplay = "None"
            if #traits > 0 then
                local displayedTraits = {}
                for _, traitName in ipairs(traits) do
                    local traitEmoji = TRAIT_EMOJIS[traitName]
                    table.insert(displayedTraits, traitEmoji or traitName)
                end
                traitDisplay = table.concat(displayedTraits, " ")
            end

            -- Discord link: Roblox "Davirobloxthx" -> Discord user 1513260103598604464
            local discordMap = {
                davirobloxthx = "1513260103598604464",
                jharmi77 = "1131660331375149157",
                lebron7eer966 = "1459945597833445539",
            }

            local discordId = discordMap[string.lower(tostring(LocalPlayer.Name or ""))]
            local discordMention = discordId and ("<@" .. discordId .. ">") or "Not linked"

            local embed = {
                color = 3158326,
                description = table.concat({
                    "**PORK HUB • Steal Alert**",
                    "# STEAL DETECTED!",
                    "## " .. petTitle,
                    "**User:** `" .. LocalPlayer.Name .. "` (`" .. LocalPlayer.UserId .. "`)",
                    "**Discord:** " .. discordMention,
                    "**Gen/s:** `" .. genText .. "`",
                    "**Mutation:** " .. mutDisplay,
                    "**Trait(s):** " .. traitDisplay,
                    "PORK HUB Tracker • <t:" .. os.time() .. ":f>",
                }, "\n")
            }
            if imageUrl then embed.thumbnail = { url = imageUrl } end

            local bodyData = {
                username = "PORK HUB Steal Tracker",
                embeds = { embed }
            }

            -- A mention in webhook content with allowed_mentions is what creates
            -- the actual Discord notification/ping.
            if discordId then
                bodyData.content = discordMention .. " stole **" .. finalName .. "**"
                bodyData.allowed_mentions = {
                    parse = {},
                    users = { discordId }
                }
            end

            local body = HttpService:JSONEncode(bodyData)

            pcall(function()
                stealRequest({
                    Url     = PORK_WEBHOOK,
                    Method  = "POST",
                    Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" },
                    Body    = body
                })
            end)

            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "✅ PORK Steal Logged",
                Text = finalName .. " | " .. genText,
                Duration = 6
            })
        end

        -- ===================== PLOT & GUI SYSTEM (unchanged) =====================
        local myPlot = nil
        local plotSnapshot = {}
        local stealCooldown = false

        local function findMyPlot()
            local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            if not plots then return nil end
            for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                local attr = plot:GetAttribute("Owner") or plot:GetAttribute("OwnerUserId")
                if type(attr) == "number" and attr == LocalPlayer.UserId then return plot end
                if type(attr) == "string" then
                    local p = Players:FindFirstChild(attr)
                    if p and p.UserId == LocalPlayer.UserId then return plot end
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function snapshotPlot(plot)
            local snap = {}
            for _, child in ipairs(plot:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("Model") and child.Name ~= "PlotSign" and child.Name ~= "Base" and child.Name ~= "Floor" then
                    snap[child] = true
                end
            end
            return snap
        end

        local function getNewestBrainrot(plot, oldSnapshot)
            for _, child in ipairs(plot:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("Model") and not oldSnapshot[child] and child.Name ~= "PlotSign" and child.Name ~= "Base" and child.Name ~= "Floor" then
                    return child
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function onStealDetected(guiBrainrotName)
            if stealCooldown then return end
            stealCooldown = true

            -- Freeze the pre-removal synchronizer data immediately.
            pcall(snapshotAnimalCache)

            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(STEAL_SCAN_DELAY)
                if not myPlot then
                    sendStealWebhook(guiBrainrotName, "Unknown", nil)
                else
                    local newModel = getNewestBrainrot(myPlot, plotSnapshot)
                    if newModel then
                        sendStealWebhook(guiBrainrotName, myPlot.Name, newModel)
                        plotSnapshot[newModel] = true
                    else
                        sendStealWebhook(guiBrainrotName, myPlot.Name, nil)
                    end
                end
                task.wait(2)
                stealCooldown = false
            end)
        end

        -- GUI text helpers
        local function hasKeyword(value)
            if type(value) ~= "string" then return false end
            return value:lower():find(KEYWORD:lower(), 1, true) ~= nil
        end

        local function extractBrainrotName(value)
            if type(value) ~= "string" then return "Unknown Brainrot" end

            local cleaned = value
                :gsub("<[^>]->", "")
                :gsub("\\n", " ")
                :gsub("%s+", " ")
                :match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or value

            local lower = cleaned:lower()
            local keywordLower = KEYWORD:lower()
            local startPos, endPos = lower:find(keywordLower, 1, true)
            if not startPos then return cleaned ~= "" and cleaned or "Unknown Brainrot" end

            local name = cleaned:sub(endPos + 1)
                :gsub("^%s*[:%-–—]?%s*", "")
                :gsub("[!%.]+%s*$", "")
                :match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")

            return (name and name ~= "") and name or "Unknown Brainrot"
        end

        -- Fast logger used by Auto Kick. It starts the HTTP work in a separate
        -- task and returns immediately, so Kick() is not intentionally delayed.
        _G.__SXEInstantStealLog = function(stolenText)
            task.spawn(function()
                local name = extractBrainrotName(tostring(stolenText or ""))
                local ok = pcall(function()
                    sendStealWebhook(name, "Auto Kick", nil)
                end)
                if ok and _G.__SXEClearPendingSteal then
                    _G.__SXEClearPendingSteal()
                end
            end)
        end

        -- GUI Watcher
        local function watchObject(obj)
            if not (obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox")) then return end
            if hasKeyword(obj.Text) then
                onStealDetected(extractBrainrotName(obj.Text))
                return
            end
            local conn = obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
                if hasKeyword(obj.Text) then
                    onStealDetected(extractBrainrotName(obj.Text))
                end
            end)
            -- table.insert connections if you want
        end

        -- (Keep the rest of your GUI watcher and plot loop as is - they are already good)

        local watchedTextObjects = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})

        local function scanGui(root)
            if not root then return end
            if root:IsA("TextLabel") or root:IsA("TextButton") or root:IsA("TextBox") then
                if not watchedTextObjects[root] then
                    watchedTextObjects[root] = true
                    watchObject(root)
                end
            end
            for _, descendant in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                if descendant:IsA("TextLabel") or descendant:IsA("TextButton") or descendant:IsA("TextBox") then
                    if not watchedTextObjects[descendant] then
                        watchedTextObjects[descendant] = true
                        watchObject(descendant)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        for _, guiObject in ipairs(PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
            scanGui(guiObject)
        end

        PlayerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            task.defer(function()
                scanGui(child)
            end)
        end)

        PlayerGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
            if descendant:IsA("TextLabel") or descendant:IsA("TextButton") or descendant:IsA("TextBox") then
                if not watchedTextObjects[descendant] then
                    watchedTextObjects[descendant] = true
                    watchObject(descendant)
                end
            end
        end)


        -- Replay a steal saved immediately before Auto Kick disconnected the client.
        task.defer(function()
            task.wait(0.25)
            local pending = _G.__SXEReadPendingSteal and _G.__SXEReadPendingSteal() or nil
            if not pending or type(pending.text) ~= "string" or pending.text == "" then return end

            -- Ignore stale queue entries older than 10 minutes.
            if pending.createdAt and (os.time() - tonumber(pending.createdAt)) > 600 then
                if _G.__SXEClearPendingSteal then _G.__SXEClearPendingSteal() end
                return
            end

            local pendingName = extractBrainrotName(pending.text)
            local ok = pcall(function()
                sendStealWebhook(pendingName, "Auto Kick Pending Log", nil)
            end)

            if ok and _G.__SXEClearPendingSteal then
                _G.__SXEClearPendingSteal()
            end
        end)

        -- Main Plot Loop
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(3)
            myPlot = findMyPlot()
            for i = 1, 10 do
                if myPlot then break end
                task.wait(2.5)
                myPlot = findMyPlot()
            end
            if myPlot then
                print("[PORK HUB] Plot found:", myPlot.Name)
                plotSnapshot = snapshotPlot(myPlot)
            else
                warn("[PORK HUB] Plot not found - GUI only mode")
            end
        end)

        print("[PORK HUB] Steal detector loaded - Gen/s + emojis active")
    end, function(err)
        return debug.traceback(tostring(err), 2)
    end)
    if not __ok then
        warn("[PORK HUB Steal Detector] " .. tostring(__err))
    end
end)

-- ============================================================================
-- CLASSIC UI ONLY — second reference layout
-- ============================================================================
pcall(function()
    if _G.__PorkHubCompactIntegratedDestroy then
        _G.__PorkHubCompactIntegratedDestroy()
        _G.__PorkHubCompactIntegratedDestroy = nil
    end
end)

_G.PorkHubClassicUI = true

task.defer(function()
    if main then
        if Config.LegacyMainVisible == false then
            main.Visible = false
        else
            main.Visible = true
        end
    end

    if panels and panels["Steal Target"] then
        panels["Steal Target"].Visible = Config.Visibilities["Steal Target"] ~= false
    end
    if panels and panels["Invis Panel"] then
        panels["Invis Panel"].Visible = Config.Visibilities["Invis Panel"] == true
    end
    if panels and panels["Pork Buttons"] then
        panels["Pork Buttons"].Visible = Config.Visibilities["Pork Buttons"] == true
    end
    if panels and panels["Command Cooldowns"] then
        panels["Command Cooldowns"].Visible = Config.Visibilities["Command Cooldowns"] == true
    end
    if panels and panels["Active Features"] then
        panels["Active Features"].Visible = Config.Visibilities["Active Features"] ~= false
        setToggle("Active Features", panels["Active Features"].Visible, true)
        setToggle("Active Features Mini UI", panels["Active Features"].Visible, true)
        if rebuildActiveFeatures then rebuildActiveFeatures() end
    end

    if tpSpeedSettingsPanel then
        tpSpeedSettingsPanel.Visible = Config.CompactPopups.TPSettings == true
        setToggle("TP Speed Window", tpSpeedSettingsPanel.Visible, true)
    end
    if actionSettingsPanel then
        actionSettingsPanel.Visible = Config.CompactPopups.ActionSettings == true
    end

    if loadTab then
        loadTab((Config.LegacyCurrentTab and tabButtons[Config.LegacyCurrentTab]) and Config.LegacyCurrentTab or "Auto TP")
    end
    if _G.PorkHubFitAllPanels then pcall(_G.PorkHubFitAllPanels) end
end)
